## 0. Integrity Statement and Plan Status

This is a planning and architecture artifact. It replaces the prior unregistered bulk-generated section in this exact path; it does not authorize production, authored-data, Core, host, UI, save, test, generated-index, or runtime edits. Every current path named below is evidence captured during this rebase. A future `MODIFY` or `CREATE` action is a proposal for a separately claimed package, never a silent instruction to the next builder.

The historical baseline was 4,992 characters in Git `HEAD`. The current working-tree file is being rebuilt from live source, live JSON, current ledgers, and the read-only compiled authority. Character count is verified externally after writing. The quality sequence is: premise correction → integration architecture → code-seam precision → deep polish → final reaccuracy → QA.

### Evidence labels

- **VERIFIED CURRENT:** path exists and was read in this rebase; the cited declaration, row, or hash is current at capture time.
- **HISTORICAL RECORD:** an older ledger/closeout says a package once landed; it is not a fresh test result.
- **INFERENCE:** a likely route supported by adjacent current seams; it still requires a claim and focused proof.
- **PROPOSAL:** a future design direction, not a current API.
- **UNKNOWN:** deliberately unresolved; no fallback fact is invented.

# 1. Objective

Make the first-hour and long-session UX contract truthful across onboarding, accessibility, keyboard/controller focus, localization-ready onboarding text, and the current host surfaces. The historical plan is a broad 14A/14B/14C proposal; current source has a persisted onboarding journey, settings/accessibility owners, 22-action input map, focus navigation, UI audits, and active related claims. The next outcome is a bounded current census and prioritized residual plan, not a mass UI rewrite or a parallel tutorial system.

**Bounded outcome:** Audit OnboardingJourney/OnboardingSaveState, Main.Onboarding, Tutorial/Onboarding panels, UserSettings/Accessibility, focus/input/modal/lifecycle surfaces, localization scaffolding, UI accessibility tests, and current route/player-surface ownership. Separate already-sealed work from real gaps and keep shared composition roots untouched unless separately claimed.

**Non-goals:** no new tutorial authority, no duplicate settings store, no mass theme rewrite, no input-map fork, no generated snapshot rebaseline, no production/UI/test/generated-index edits in this planning package

# 2. Current Decision and Terminal/Residual Status

- VERIFIED CURRENT: `OnboardingJourney` owns stages, sigils, hints, assistance, completion, and capture/restore.
- VERIFIED CURRENT: `onboarding` is a registered save section with a checksummed host store.
- VERIFIED CURRENT: input/focus/accessibility/settings owners and focused tests exist.
- The plan must treat currently active PFGL/UI claims and shared roots as read-only until ownership is rechecked.
- HISTORICAL RECORD: the original Plan 14 describes broad tasks and historical UI counts; those counts and skill paths are drift-prone and not reused without verification.

The safe planning decision is not “grow the old number.” It is:

1. preserve the current owner and data contract;
2. identify what the historical plan already completed;
3. separate dormant content, display-only vocabulary, and false reachability from live commands;
4. define the smallest residual integration package, if one is justified; and
5. leave a builder with exact paths and focused verification rather than fictional APIs.

**Master-authority application:**

- The Factory Protocol requires a premise sweep, one bounded lane/cluster, collision checking, evidence labels, and a continuity check.
- The generator matrices and deep maps require current C1–C17 ownership rather than a historical title.
- The save/state lane requires capture/restore/migration and determinism to be explicit.
- The UI/accessibility lane requires truthful presentation, words-not-color-only, focus, controller parity, and lifecycle evidence.
- The verification lane requires focused tests and two-pass determinism where a simulation exists.
- The anti-padding rule forbids manufacturing 600-day traces, 100-test suites, repeated dossiers, or “sealed” language merely to increase size.

```text
Authored JSON / current owner state
              │
              ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ UX, Onboarding, Accessibility, and Input: First-Hour Truth, Thousandth-Hour Reachability                                               │

└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              ▼
Current loader/validator → current Core owner → existing host seam
              │

              ├─ existing save owner and checksum/codec
              ├─ Godot presenter/projection (no gameplay arithmetic)
              └─ focused tests + headless evidence
```

### 6.1 Architectural decisions

1. **OnboardingJourney owns stages/state.**
2. **Main.Onboarding binds and persists.**
3. **Settings own preferences.**
4. **Input/focus own navigation.**
5. **Panels project truth.**

**Single-owner test:** before any future change, search for another mutable collection, catalog copy, save field, event producer, or UI cache claiming the same concern. A duplicate is a blocker or an explicit projection, never a convenience authority.

# 8. Data Flow

1. load current settings/accessibility and input contracts
2. construct/restore the existing onboarding journey
3. observe canonical sigils/failures/day from current host seams
4. advance or skip stages through OnboardingJourney
5. present the current hint/tutorial and focus state through UI
6. persist onboarding/settings through their existing stores and verify recovery/lifecycle

Every arrow is one-way for authority. A presenter may call a command, but the resulting state must return through the owner mutation/event. No view-local “temporary truth” may become a save fact.

# 9. State Model and Invariants

- onboarding completion/dismissal/assistance survive save/load
- a hint appears only after its current trigger and can be dismissed/replayed
- focus traversal and controller close/back work in every modal state
- settings are applied through UserSettings/accessibility owners, not panel fields
- localization keys are stable and source strings are not silently invented
- a first-hour funnel measurement never mutates gameplay state
- snapshot/a11y evidence is current and renderer-capable when visual output changes

**Invariant review questions:**

- Which values are authored definitions, which are player decisions, and which are derived projections?
- What is the exact identity key and ordering rule?
- What happens on missing, duplicate, stale, malformed, or legacy input?
- Which transitions are monotonic, bounded, idempotent, or exactly-once?
- Which state survives save/load, and which is recomputed after restore?

# 10. API and Contract Design

The current declaration indexes in Appendix C are authoritative for method names. The following is a future contract shape, not a claim that every method already exists:

```text
Preview(command, context) -> named availability/refusal + exact deltas
Execute(command, context) -> owner mutation + typed fact
QueryCurrentState(subjectId) -> read-only projection
CaptureState() -> deep serializable owner state
RestoreState(legacyOrCurrentState) -> normalized owner state
```

Contract rules for UX/onboarding/accessibility surface:

- Refusal is named and stable; no silent default success.
- Unknown ids remain unknown or are rejected with a diagnostic, according to the current loader contract.
- Preview and execute use the same gate calculation; UI cannot bypass a prerequisite.
- Events are emitted after the owning mutation commits and before presentation refresh.
- Any repeated event has an explicit idempotency key or a documented at-most-once policy.

# 11. Data Plan and Catalog Authority

`project.godot`, UserSettings, accessibility profiles, onboarding state, and localization tables are current authorities. The original plan mentions skills that may not exist or may be historical; verify each tool/script path before citing it. Do not add a second tutorial catalog or hardcoded string registry.

The JSON data authority remains under `Assets/StreamingAssets/Data/`. A future row requires a schema/version decision, stable id, bounded fields, a named consumer, validation, continuity review, and a focused test. Text must describe modeled state and must not invent mechanics.

# 12. Save, Restore, and Migration

Use the existing `onboarding` section and UserSettings/accessibility persistence. Tutorial dismissal, completion, and assistance are onboarding state; accessibility preferences remain settings owners. A new UI preference must not be stored in a panel cache or a duplicate settings file.

**Save proof matrix:** current owner state → deep capture → serialize → restore to a fresh instance → continue the same action sequence → compare state, ordering, and checksum/fingerprint. A catalog test or snapshot does not substitute for this matrix. Legacy input must produce the documented neutral/default state, never an invented favorable outcome.

# 13. Determinism and Replay

Onboarding state transitions are deterministic for the same sigil/failure/day sequence. Telemetry/funnel measurements are read-only and must not consume gameplay RNG. Input/focus ordering is ordinal and UI refresh must not change stage state.

**Replay proof:** same seed, catalog version, command sequence, and save fixture produce the same ordered ids, events, state transitions, and visible projection. If a new random decision is genuinely required, use an existing seeded stream or a deliberately forked `CampaignRngManager` stream; never use wall-clock time, hash iteration order, or `System.Random` in deterministic Core behavior.

# 14. System and Event Wiring

OnboardingJourney records sigils/stage transitions; Main.Onboarding projects hints and saves. Gameplay owners emit existing facts; the tutorial never changes needs, radiation, power, or inventory. Accessibility settings emit their current preference-change seam and UI re-renders without mutating gameplay.

**Event ordering:** owner mutation → canonical fact/event → host consumer → UI projection → dirty-save flush. A host adapter may translate an owner fact into a canonical consequence only through the owning system’s existing API. Optional presentation may be absent; it may not fabricate a live command.

# 15. Godot Host Integration

**Current host surfaces:**

- `src/Main.Onboarding.cs` — current onboarding setup, sigil observation, hints, and persistence
- `src/Host/OnboardingSaveStore.cs` — checksummed onboarding state
- `src/UI/TutorialPanel.cs` — current tutorial presentation
- `src/UI/OnboardingHintPanel.cs` — contextual hint presentation
- `src/UI/AshfallFocusNavigator.cs` — spatial focus traversal
- `src/UI/ModalManager.cs` — modal close/back behavior
- `src/Host/UiAccessibilitySelfTest.cs` — accessibility/lifecycle evidence
- `src/Main.PanelLifecycle.cs` — shared lifecycle cleanup

The Godot layer is limited to composition, input, routing, binding, refresh, accessibility, audio/visual presentation, and lifecycle cleanup. Shared `Main`/panel/save composition roots are integrator-owned and must be claimed exactly before an implementation change.

**UI truth contract:** show the current owner’s value, source, availability, refusal, and next consequence. Use text/icon/shape in addition to color. Preserve close/back, focus traversal, controller navigation, reduced motion, and truthful empty/loading/error states.

# 16. Narrative and Content Integration

Onboarding text is a truthful map from current mechanics to current actions. It may explain a system, but it cannot grant supplies, suppress a failure, pre-complete a stage, or promise a feature that is not reachable. Localization scaffolding must preserve stable keys and interpolation placeholders.

Content must remain fictional, restrained, human, and grounded in the actual model. A record may describe an event only if the event system can produce it. Do not use prose to smuggle in a new resource, faction, casualty, relationship, or ending.

# 18. Test Strategy

The implementation owner should run the smallest target first, then only directly affected regional tests. The planning package does not claim these commands were freshly executed.

### Focused Core/data targets

1. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/OnboardingJourneyTests.cs`
2. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/UI/OnboardingWiringGateTests.cs`
3. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Tooling/InputMapContractTests.cs`
4. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Tooling/UiAccessibilityGateTests.cs`
5. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Settings/UserSettingsRecoveryTests.cs`
6. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/UI/AccessibilitySourceAuditTests.cs`

### Permanent/static checks

1. `godot --headless --path . -- --data-integrity-selftest`
2. `godot --headless --path . -- --content-utilization-selftest` (only when the current catalog is in the utilization scope)
3. `git diff --check -- <owned planning paths>`

### Verification layers

- **Data:** schema, id uniqueness, bounded fields, references, and catalog admission.
- **Core:** pure behavior, boundaries, stable ordering, malformed/legacy inputs, and event emission.
- **Persistence:** capture/restore/deep-copy, old-save default, checksum/codec, and exactly-once fields.
- **Determinism:** paired same-seed replay and, if a stream is involved, two-run fingerprint equality.
- **Host:** setup, command, save, restore, reset, and lifecycle wiring.
- **UI:** truthful projection, focus, controller/back, accessibility, refresh, and no fabricated fallback.
- **Runtime:** only the smallest relevant headless flag; no broad suite by default.

# 19. Dependency-Ordered Implementation Phases

| Phase | Outcome | Completion gate | Forbidden shortcut |
|---|---|---|---|
| Phase 0 — current surface census | read onboarding, settings, input, focus, panels, lifecycle, and gates | all active/claimed/shared seams are classified | no undocumented scope or shortcut |
| Phase 1 — first-hour trace | follow fresh boot → sigils → stages → hints → day/save | each demanded mechanic has a teach/demand point | no undocumented scope or shortcut |
| Phase 2 — accessibility/input audit | check focus, controller, reduced motion, text, recovery, and lifecycle | no panel-local authority | no undocumented scope or shortcut |
| Phase 3 — bounded residual | only a proven UX/accessibility defect is promoted | exact shared paths and focused tests | no undocumented scope or shortcut |
| Phase 4 — renderer/snapshot closeout | only for visual changes on a renderer-capable session | no headless fabricated snapshot | no undocumented scope or shortcut |

**First safe implementation step:** Phase 0 is a read-only current census. No phase starts by creating a type named only in the historical baseline. If the owner, save path, loader schema, or event seam differs from this plan, return `STALE_PLAN` and update the claim.

# 21. Risks and Mitigations

| Risk | Control / stop condition |
|---|---|
| shared-root race | claim exact shared paths after current claims settle |
| tutorial becomes gameplay authority | keep hints read-only |
| a11y regression hidden by headless tests | use appropriate gates and renderer evidence |
| settings/onboarding state divergence | use existing stores and recovery tests |

# 22. Explicit Non-Goals

- no new tutorial authority, no duplicate settings store, no mass theme rewrite, no input-map fork, no generated snapshot rebaseline, no production/UI/test/generated-index edits in this planning package

# 23. Rollback and Recovery

- This planning-only change is reversible by restoring the prior version of the exact plan path; no runtime rollback is required because no production, data, test, UI, save, or generated-index file is changed here.
- A future implementation must keep the prior valid owner state and catalog schema available until its focused migration/round-trip target passes.
- If a new owner, codec, event seam, or shared composition root is required, stop and return `STALE_PLAN`/a decision packet rather than improvising a rollback for a parallel architecture.
- For a future data change, retain the prior valid JSON fixture and document whether recovery is a revert, additive default, or explicit migration. Never silently down-convert a newer state.

# 24. Definition of Done

- The current owner, data authority, host/UI boundary, save owner, determinism rule, and failure contracts for UX/onboarding/accessibility surface are named from current evidence.
- Historical expansion counts and pass claims are clearly separated from current reachability and current test declarations.
- The required residual is bounded to one future claim, or explicitly recorded as a safe no-change result when no defect survives the census.
- Every future change has an exact focused command and an owner/path claim; no count-only or filler-only acceptance remains.
- The final precision pass has rechecked hashes, paths, terminology, and unsupported claims.

**DoD is behavioral:** a builder can execute the first safe step without reinterpreting ownership, and a reviewer can reject any shortcut that creates a second authority or treats catalog presence as live reachability.

# 25. Implementation Handoff Contract

## MUST PRESERVE

- Godot as the only active engine; Core remains engine-free.
- Current JSON/data owners and current save/codec owners.
- Deterministic streams, campaign-day semantics, and existing event ordering.
- Existing accessibility, controller, focus, and lifecycle contracts.
- Sealed, retired, accepted, blocked, and active decisions in the live ledgers.

## MUST ADD ONLY AFTER A NEW CLAIM

- A current first-hour teach-versus-demand trace.
- A current accessibility/input/focus/lifecycle census with active-claim boundaries.
- A prioritized, bounded residual list rather than a broad rewrite.

## MUST NOT DO

- create a second onboarding/tutorial system
- add a duplicate settings store
- edit shared roots without transfer
- call headless output visual proof

## VERIFY WITH

1. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/OnboardingJourneyTests.cs`
2. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/UI/OnboardingWiringGateTests.cs`
3. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Tooling/InputMapContractTests.cs`
4. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Tooling/UiAccessibilityGateTests.cs`
5. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/Settings/UserSettingsRecoveryTests.cs`
6. `bash scripts/run_test.sh Ashfall.Core.Tests/UI/AccessibilitySourceAuditTests.cs`

## FIRST SAFE IMPLEMENTATION STEP

Phase 0: read OnboardingJourney, Main.Onboarding, Tutorial/Onboarding panels, UserSettings/Accessibility, input/focus/modal, lifecycle, and the focused tests; record active ownership and current routes.

00078: ### 2.1 The seven-step factory loop
00079:
00080: **Step 1 — Premise sweep (mandatory, every time).**
00081: Before selecting any candidate, the session re-verifies premises against live source: the live `Assets/StreamingAssets/Data/` listing (duplication firewall, DR-04), `INTEGRATION_PLANS.md` current batch (DR-06), `WORKTREE_OWNERSHIP.md` claims (DR-09), `KNOWN_DEBT.md`, the root coordination files (DR-01), `docs/gaps/` and `docs/incidents/` (DR-02), and `docs/plans/UNCLAIMED_CORPUS_CENSUS.md` (DR-08). Output: a short premise sheet. A candidate whose premise fails the sweep is discarded, not patched.
00082:
00083: **Step 2 — Select exactly one lane and one subsystem cluster.**
00084: From Part III. The selection rule is lane rotation discipline (v1.0 Part 11): at most one plan per lane per wave; data-first lanes (A, C, J) precede wiring lanes (B, D, E) within the same domain. The session states the lane and cluster in the plan header.
00085:
00086: **Step 3 — Pull the cell's opening archetype and instantiate it.**
00087: Each matrix cell names an archetype (the *kind* of expansion that cell supports, with its owning seams). The session instantiates the archetype against current evidence: which catalog, which loader, which host session, which save family, which panel. If the archetype's seams no longer exist as described, the cell is stale — record the correction in the Drift Register and pick again.
00088:
00089: **Step 4 — Draft the subject plan in the v2.0 subject-plan format (Part V, Template S).**
00090: A subject plan is not an integration plan. It states WHAT should expand, WHY (with evidence), WHAT MUST NOT CHANGE, and WHICH INTEGRATION ROUTE the repository should prefer — but it does not prescribe line-level implementation. The integration route recommendation (Part V, Template R) names the tier (data-only / host wiring / Core extension), the seams, the save impact class, and the verification class. This preserves the repo's own separation: subject plans propose; integration plans (drafted later, against the live tree, in an owning session) commit.

00242: ## PART IV — SEEDED EXPANSION BACKLOG (AUDIT-DERIVED CANDIDATES)
00243:

00245:

### Authority lines 324–337
00324: ## PART VI — MULTI-SESSION GROWTH PROTOCOL (THE HONEST PATH TO 2,000,000 CHARACTERS)
00325:
00326: The v1.0 bible is roughly 86,000 characters of verified reference. A two-million-character corpus is roughly twenty-three times that volume. That volume is reachable, but only as accumulated *verified* content, because the repository's constitution forbids manufactured padding and this document inherits that rule. The protocol:
00327:
00328: 1. **Volume unit.** A volume is one appended Part to this document (or one of its companion canvases) produced in a single session, typically 15,000–60,000 characters, always evidence-grounded against the live repository.
00329: 2. **Volume types, in rotation:** (a) subsystem deep-map volumes (one per cluster C1–C17: full catalog inventories, prose-coverage gaps, seam maps); (b) prose specification libraries (expanded Part 9 field contracts with worked examples per document genre); (c) backlog replenishment volumes (fresh premise sweeps converting new Drift Register entries into SB-candidates); (d) lane deep guides (one per lane: full archetype playbooks with worked subject plans); (e) audit volumes (periodic re-audits refreshing the Drift Register).
00330: 3. **Session checklist.** Each session: run the Step 1 premise sweep; execute the Factory Protocol or append a volume; update the Drift Register for anything that moved; record the character count and volume index in the growth ledger below.
00331: 4. **Growth ledger.** v2.0 base: approximately 25,000 characters (this document). Target: 2,000,000. Every appended volume appends one ledger line: `[date] Volume [n] ([type]) — [chars] — cumulative [total]`.
00332: 5. **Quality gates that never relax:** every substantive statement carries a fact status; every volume names its verification surface; no volume may open a sealed surface (DR-06) or a decision-blocked item without the named signature; no volume may duplicate a live catalog or system.
00333: 6. **Anti-padding rule.** If a session cannot find verified content for the next volume, it records "no warranted volume" and stops. Zero-volume sessions are acceptable outcomes under the repository's own zero-plans doctrine.
00334:
00335: ---

00929: ## 3.6 Subsystem deep maps (D-01 through D-17; maps, not verdicts)
00930:
00931: Each map lists the cluster's canon owners, live catalogs, host sessions, and current factory openings. These are planning instruments: a session picks a cluster, reads its map, and consumes its seeds. All catalog and system names below are carried from the verified v1.0 inventory and the live 2026-09-24 listings; per-field internals remain session-verify territory.
00932:
00933: **DM-1 — Shelter operations (C1).** Owners: shelter rooms/identities/machines, thermal, schedules, social events, decor, fire, noise, airlock security, decon, atmosphere, sanitation (power-fed). # Appendix H/I/J — Deep polishing and final precision passes

# Appendix H — Deep polishing pass 1: content, premise, and evidence depth

**Pass intent:** improve `UX, Onboarding, Accessibility, and Input: First-Hour Truth, Thousandth-Hour Reachability` without inflating row counts or reopening sealed architecture. The pass asks whether every historical verb (“expand”, “wire”, “save”, “autonomous”, “completed”) matches a current declaration, caller, or explicitly labeled residual.

## H.1 Content corrections
- Do not repeat historical “265 UI files/69 snapshots” without current count proof.
- Do not assume every named audit skill/script exists or is the correct current instrument.

## H.2 Evidence-strength corrections
- Separate current sealed surfaces from stale historical counts.
- Trace first-hour demand versus teaching.
- Protect shared roots and renderer evidence.

## H.3 Anti-filler gate
- Remove generated “100 tests”, “600-day trace”, fictional dossiers, and repeated variants unless the named current file or catalog actually contains the corresponding evidence.
- A long source appendix is acceptable only when every included file is a current owner, loader, host, UI, data, or focused-test seam. It is not permission to duplicate the same file or paste unrelated code.
- Keep historical ledger claims in a historical column. Never convert an old PASS count into a current verification statement.

# Appendix I — Deep polishing pass 2: integration architecture and code seams

**Pass intent:** make the next builder’s route executable for UX, Onboarding, Accessibility, and Input: First-Hour Truth, Thousandth-Hour Reachability while preserving one authority per concern. The route is data → loader/validator → Core owner → existing save section → host adapter → event/fact → UI projection → focused verification.

## I.1 Architectural decisions
- Use OnboardingJourney and OnboardingSaveState for onboarding state.
- Use Main.Onboarding/OnboardingSaveStore for host/persistence.
- Use UserSettings/AccessibilitySettingsSystem for preferences.
- Use input/focus/modal owners for navigation.
- Use UI panels as projections and lifecycle participants.

## I.2 Host and presentation contract
- The Godot layer may compose `the current host owner`, bind providers, route commands, and render truthful state. It may not reimplement ux, onboarding, accessibility, and input: first-hour truth, thousandth-hour reachability arithmetic or persist a shadow copy.
- Shared panel registries, `Main` composition roots, save orchestrators, and generated indexes remain integrator-owned unless a future package claims them exactly.

## I.3 Code-level seam checklist
- Confirm the exact current public method and field names from the declaration indexes in Appendix C before writing code.
- Confirm the current save section/store and restore path by reading the owner and its host façade; do not infer persistence from a `CaptureState` method alone.
- Confirm event ordering and exactly-once semantics at the first mutation edge; a panel refresh is not an event producer.
- Keep deterministic collections ordinal-stable, use existing `ISeededRng` streams only where the owner already requires randomness, and use invariant formatting for checksums.

# Appendix J — Final precision, reaccuracy, and full repolishing phase

This pass is intentionally performed after the architecture pass. It re-reads the current source/data hashes, checks every named path, removes stale terminology, downgrades unsupported claims, and records the exact bounded residual. It is the final full repolishing phase: it does not add scope, but it does reconcile the entire plan against current authority before handoff.

## J.1 Final corrections applied
- No broad UI rewrite is authorized.
- Every future shared-path edit requires exact ownership transfer.

## J.2 Questions deliberately left open
- Which current onboarding stage has the earliest unavoidable failure?
- Which accessibility preference lacks an observable current surface?
- When can renderer-capable snapshot rebaseline run without competing with active claims?

## J.3 Handoff acceptance
- The next agent can identify the sole owner, the safe extension route, the existing save owner, the host/UI boundary, the deterministic contract, and the focused commands without reinterpreting this document.
- If any open question becomes a new architecture decision, stop and return `STALE_PLAN` or a decision packet rather than improvising.

# Appendix K — Quality-assurance record

- Evidence source order was applied: current repository first, project instructions second, compiled authority third, ledgers fourth.
- No production/data/test/UI/save/runtime file was edited by this documentation-only package.
- Source hashes and path existence are rechecked externally after generation; a stale hash is a failed handoff, not a historical footnote.
- Current test declarations are enumerated, but no fresh test pass is implied by enumeration.
- The plan separates terminal content expansion from any residual reachability or integration work.
- Size is a gate, not a quality proxy: the document must remain navigable, evidence-linked, and free of repeated generated filler.

# Appendix L — Original baseline intent (Git HEAD, preserved)

# Appendix L — Original baseline intent (Git HEAD, preserved)

The original file at `HEAD:piagentsplans/14-ux-onboarding-accessibility.md` contained 4,992 characters. It is retained as provenance, not as current implementation authority. The generated working-tree expansion is superseded by this rebase.

```markdown
# Plan 14 — UX, Onboarding & Accessibility: The First Hour and the Thousandth

> **Theme:** 265 UI files, a fixed 1920×1080 viewport, 69 golden snapshots — and known gaps in
> onboarding, accessibility, and information clarity. This plan makes the game *learnable* and
> *readable* without touching a single survival mechanic.
>
> **Key evidence:** `docs/HoldfastManualPlaytest.md`, `docs/ui/`, 69 snapshot targets;
> skills `ashfall-tutorial-review`, `ashfall-ui-access`, `ashfall-input-map-audit`,
> `ashfall-string-extractor` exist because these are known needs.

---

## Task 14A — First-hour onboarding & tutorial review

**Goal:** Audit and fix the first hour so a new player understands needs, radiation, and the
daily tick before the game punishes them for not knowing.

**Files:** `src/UI/` TutorialPanel + HUD overlays, starting-state data, read-only
`docs/HoldfastManualPlaytest.md`, `NeedsSystem`, `RadiationSystem`.

**Substeps:**
1. Run skill `ashfall-tutorial-review` to produce the evidence report: what the first hour demands vs. what it teaches.
2. Play/trace the first 10 ticks headless (`ashfall-telemetry-playtest`) harvesting need/morale/radiation KPIs to find the first unavoidable failure.
3. Inventory TutorialPanel content vs. the 8 core needs + radiation + power/water triage.
4. Identify the 3 teach-vs-demand gaps (things demanded before taught).
5. Author/fix tutorial steps to close those 3 gaps (teach radiation dosimeter reading, teach ration policy, teach power triage).
6. Add contextual first-time hints (first fallout storm, first sickness, first expedition) as dismissible overlays.
7. Ensure tutorial state persists (dismissed stays dismissed) via existing save pattern.
8. Adjust starting inventory/grace so the first hour is tense but survivable (data-only).
9. Snapshot-diff the tutorial/HUD panels; approve deliberately.
10. Re-run telemetry playtest → first-hour funnel shows no unavoidable early death.

**Next steps:** a "field manual" codex tab (uses JournalCodex); difficulty presets for grace period.

---

## Task 14B — Accessibility & readability sweep

**Goal:** Bring the fixed-1080p UI up to accessibility baseline: contrast, overflow, scaling,
keyboard navigation, and text readability across all 265 UI files.

**Files:** `src/UI/` broadly, `project.godot` (display/input), theme resources; output report
to `docs/ui/ACCESSIBILITY_AUDIT.md`.

**Substeps:**
1. Run `ashfall-ui-access` to generate the evidence audit (contrast, overflow, scaling, keyboard nav).
2. Fix contrast failures in the theme (the two fonts: BarlowCondensed + ShareTechMono at small sizes are the likely offenders).
3. Fix text overflow on the highest-traffic panels (Survivors, Medical, Inventory, Map) at default and scaled sizes.
4. Add UI scale setting (100/125/150%) if absent; verify fixed-viewport handling.
5. Audit keyboard/controller navigation: tab order, focus indicators, panel shortcuts.
6. Add colorblind-safe treatment to danger/radiation indicators (icon + color, never color alone).
7. Add font-size floor for critical readouts (dosimeter, health).
8. Run `ashfall-input-map-audit` for binding conflicts and rebind gaps.
9. Re-render + snapshot-diff all 69 golden panels; review every diff visually.
10. Document baseline + fixes in `ACCESSIBILITY_AUDIT.md`; add a contrast regression check if tooling allows.

**Next steps:** localization prep (14C) benefits directly; screen-reader/one-hand play is out
of scope for 2D management but document the stance.

---

## Task 14C — Localization readiness & string extraction

**Goal:** Prepare the game for translation: inventory hardcoded user-facing strings, scaffold
the Godot translation layer, and gate new hardcoded strings.

**Files:** `src/UI/` + `.tscn` + `Assets/StreamingAssets/Data/*.json` (user-facing text),
`project.godot` (locale), new translation scaffolding; output CSV/POT inventory.

**Substeps:**
1. Run `ashfall-string-extractor` to inventory hardcoded strings across UI, scenes, and data JSON.
2. Categorize: UI labels, tutorial text, event/quest prose, item descriptions, journal/radio text.
3. Decide the localization boundary: UI labels + structured text localizable; the 272 narrative docs are a separate, later translation project (note it).
4. Scaffold Godot `TranslationServer` integration + a `res://` locale resource structure.
5. Extract UI labels + structured strings into CSV/POT with stable keys.
6. Replace hardcoded UI strings with keyed lookups (panel by panel, small batches).
7. Add a locale switcher to settings (English source only to start).
8. Add a CI gate flagging new hardcoded user-facing strings in `src/UI`.
9. Verify data-JSON text fields use a consistent, extractable pattern (they're content, translated separately).
10. `dotnet build` + snapshot-diff (keyed lookups must render identically in source locale).

**Next steps:** actual translation is a content project post-release-candidate; RTL/text-expansion
UI stress test with a pseudo-locale.

```

## End of Plan 14 — current-evidence rebase

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004: using System.Linq;
00005:
00006: namespace Ashfall.Core.Onboarding
00007: {
00008:     /// <summary>
00009:     /// Authoritative definition of one onboarding stage: a stable id, the
00010:     /// observable sigils that satisfy it, and a short label + objective text.
00011:     /// No hint copy lives here — diegetic hint text is composed by the host to
00012:     /// keep Core free of presentation prose.
00013:     /// </summary>
00014:     public readonly struct OnboardingStageDef
00015:     {
00016:         public readonly OnboardingStage Id;
00017:         public readonly string Title;
00018:         public readonly string Objective;
00019:         public readonly string ShowMeWhereRoute;
00020:         public readonly (string Sigil, int Threshold)[] Requirements;
00021:
00022:         public OnboardingStageDef(
00023:             OnboardingStage id,
00024:             string title,
00025:             string objective,
00026:             string showMeWhereRoute,
00027:             params (string, int)[] requirements)
00028:         {
00029:             Id = id;
00030:             Title = title ?? string.Empty;
00031:             Objective = objective ?? string.Empty;
00032:             ShowMeWhereRoute = showMeWhereRoute ?? string.Empty;
00033:             Requirements = requirements ?? Array.Empty<(string, int)>();
00034:         }
00035:     }
00036:

00039:     /// machine iterates in declared order. Sigil names are stable across saves.

00101:             OnboardingStage.Duty,
00102:             OnboardingStage.Dose,
00103:             OnboardingStage.Research,
00104:             OnboardingStage.Expedition,
00105:         };

00155:
00156:         public static OnboardingStageDef DefFor(OnboardingProfile profile, OnboardingStage id)
00157:         {
00158:             if (profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour)
00159:             {
00160:                 for (int i = 0; i < FirstHour.Length; i++)
00161:                     if (FirstHour[i].Id == id) return FirstHour[i];
00162:                 throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(id), id, "Not a first-hour onboarding stage.");
00163:             }
00164:
00165:             return Def(id);
00166:         }

00169:             profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour ? FirstHourOrder : LegacyOrder;

00184:
00185:     /// <summary>
00186:     /// Stateful onboarding journey. Deterministic engine-agnostic machine; the
00187:     /// host supplies real observed signals via <see cref="RecordSigil"/> and
00188:     /// updates time via <see cref="SetDay"/>. The persistent sigil store is
00189:     /// an ordinal-stable list so <c>SaveChecksum</c>'s recursive walk yields the
00190:     /// same hash across runtime vs built-with-separate-process builds; the
00191:     /// runtime counter is a private Dictionary used for fast requirements
00192:     /// checks.
00193:     ///
00194:     /// Two completion surfaces coexist: <em>stageComplete</em> from real signals
00195:     /// or <see cref="SkipCurrent"/>; <em>journeyComplete</em> is reached only
00196:     /// by the profile's terminal contract (day advance for legacy, expedition
00197:     /// dispatch for first-hour), never by a clock tick alone.
00198:     /// </summary>
00199:     public sealed class OnboardingJourney
00200:     {
00201:         public const int SaveVersion = 1;
00202:
00203:         private readonly Dictionary<string, int> _counts =
00204:             new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00205:
00206:         private OnboardingSaveState _state;
00207:
00208:         public OnboardingStage CurrentStage => (OnboardingStage)_state.currentStage;
00209:
00210:         public OnboardingProfile Profile => (OnboardingProfile)_state.profile;
00211:
00212:         public bool JourneyComplete => _state.journeyComplete;
00213:
00214:         public bool IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage stage)
00215:             => _state.completedStages.Contains((int)stage);
00216:
00217:         public OnboardingAssistance Assistance => (OnboardingAssistance)_state.assistance;
00218:
00219:         public int Day => _state.day;
00220:
00221:         public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, int> Sigils => _counts;
00222:
00223:         public IReadOnlyList<int> CompletedStages =>
00224:             _state.completedStages ?? new List<int>();
00225:
00226:         public IReadOnlyList<string> DismissedHints =>
00227:             _state.dismissedHints ?? new List<string>();
00228:
00229:         /// <summary>One-shot contextual tutorial IDs already shown.</summary>
00230:         public IReadOnlyList<string> ContextualTutorialSeenIds =>
00231:             _state.contextualTutorialSeenIds ?? new List<string>();
00232:
00233:         /// <summary>Stable FIFO queue for contextual tutorial requests.</summary>
00234:         public IReadOnlyList<string> ContextualTutorialQueue =>
00235:             _state.contextualTutorialQueue ?? new List<string>();
00236:
00237:         public event Action<OnboardingStage>? OnStageAdvanced;
00238:         public event Action<OnboardingJourney>? OnJourneyChanged;
00239:         public event Action<string>? OnContextualTutorialRequested;
00240:
00241:         public OnboardingJourney()
00242:         {
00243:             _state = new OnboardingSaveState();
00244:         }
00245:
00246:         public OnboardingJourney(OnboardingProfile profile)
00247:         {
00248:             OnboardingProfile normalized = profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00249:                 ? OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00250:                 : OnboardingProfile.Legacy;
00251:             _state = new OnboardingSaveState
00252:             {
00253:                 profile = (int)normalized,
00254:                 currentStage = (int)OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(normalized)[0],
00255:             };
00256:         }
00257:
00258:         public static OnboardingJourney CreateFirstHour() =>
00259:             new OnboardingJourney(OnboardingProfile.FirstHour);
00260:
00261:         public OnboardingSignalResult RecordSigil(string sigilName, int delta = 1)
00262:         {
00263:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sigilName) || delta <= 0)
00264:                 return OnboardingSignalResult.Ignored;
00265:             if (string.Equals(sigilName, OnboardingCatalog.DaySentinel, StringComparison.Ordinal))
00266:                 return OnboardingSignalResult.Ignored;
00267:
00268:             bool progressed = false;
00269:             int prior = _counts.TryGetValue(sigilName, out int prev) ? prev : 0;
00270:             int next = prior + delta;
00271:             _counts[sigilName] = next;
00272:             if (next != prior) progressed = true;
00273:
00274:             bool newlySatisfied = AdvanceStagesAccountingForNonTerminal();
00275:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00276:
00277:             if (newlySatisfied) return OnboardingSignalResult.Advanced;
00278:             if (progressed) return OnboardingSignalResult.Progressed;
00279:             return OnboardingSignalResult.Ignored;
00280:         }
00281:
00282:         public bool SkipCurrent()
00283:         {
00284:             OnboardingStage stage = CurrentStage;
00285:             if (IsTerminalStage(stage)) return false;
00286:             int idx = (int)stage;
00287:             if (_state.completedStages.Contains(idx)) return false;
00288:
00289:             _state.completedStages.Add(idx);
00290:             OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke((OnboardingStage)idx);
00291:             _state.currentStage = NextIncompleteIndex();
00292:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00293:             return true;
00294:         }
00295:
00296:         public void SkipAllRemaining()
00297:         {
00298:             var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile);
00299:             for (int i = CurrentPosition(); i < order.Count; i++)
00300:             {
00301:                 int stage = (int)order[i];
00302:                 if (IsTerminalStage(order[i])) break;
00303:                 if (!_state.completedStages.Contains(stage))
00304:                 {
00305:                     _state.completedStages.Add(stage);
00306:                     OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke(order[i]);
00307:                 }
00308:             }
00309:             _state.currentStage = NextIncompleteIndex();
00310:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00311:         }
00312:
00313:         public void Replay()
00314:         {
00315:             _state.completedStages.Clear();
00316:             _state.stagesGuided.Clear();
00317:             _state.dismissedHints.Clear();
00318:             _state.contextualTutorialSeenIds.Clear();
00319:             _state.contextualTutorialQueue.Clear();
00320:             _counts.Clear();
00321:
00322:             if (Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour)
00323:             {
00324:                 _state.journeyComplete = false;
00325:                 _state.currentStage = (int)OnboardingCatalog.FirstHourOrder[0];
00326:             }
00327:             else if (_state.day >= 2)
00328:             {
00329:                 _state.completedStages.Add((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00330:                 _state.journeyComplete = true;
00331:                 _state.currentStage = (int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance;
00332:             }
00333:             else
00334:             {
00335:                 _state.journeyComplete = false;
00336:                 _state.currentStage = (int)OnboardingStage.Protocol;
00337:             }
00338:             OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke(CurrentStage);
00339:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00340:         }
00341:
00342:         public void SetAssistance(OnboardingAssistance level)
00343:         {
00344:             if (_state.assistance == (int)level) return;
00345:             _state.assistance = (int)level;
00346:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00347:         }
00348:
00349:         public void SetDay(int day)
00350:         {
00351:             if (day <= _state.day) return;
00352:             _state.day = day;
00353:
00354:             if (Profile == OnboardingProfile.Legacy && _state.day >= 2)
00355:             {
00356:                 if (!_state.completedStages.Contains((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance))
00357:                 {
00358:                     _state.completedStages.Add((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00359:                     OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke(OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00360:                 }
00361:                 if (!_state.journeyComplete)
00362:                 {
00363:                     _state.journeyComplete = true;
00364:                     OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke(OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00365:                 }
00366:             }
00367:             _state.currentStage = NextIncompleteIndex();
00368:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00369:         }
00370:
00371:         public void DismissHint(string hintKey)
00372:         {
00373:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hintKey)) return;
00374:             if (_state.dismissedHints.Contains(hintKey)) return;
00375:             _state.dismissedHints.Add(hintKey);
00376:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00377:         }
00378:
00379:         public bool IsHintDismissed(string hintKey)
00380:             => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(hintKey) &&
00381:                _state.dismissedHints != null &&
00382:                _state.dismissedHints.Contains(hintKey);
00383:
00384:         /// <summary>
00385:         /// Requests a contextual tutorial through the existing persisted
00386:         /// onboarding authority. The seen check happens before the event is
00387:         /// raised, so duplicate host subscriptions and duplicate domain event
00388:         /// delivery cannot show the same lesson twice.
00389:         /// </summary>
00390:         public bool RequestContextualTutorial(string tutorialId)
00391:         {
00392:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tutorialId)) return false;
00393:             _state.contextualTutorialSeenIds ??= new List<string>();
00394:             _state.contextualTutorialQueue ??= new List<string>();
00395:             if (_state.contextualTutorialSeenIds.Contains(tutorialId)) return false;
00396:
00397:             _state.contextualTutorialSeenIds.Add(tutorialId);
00398:             _state.contextualTutorialQueue.Add(tutorialId);
00399:             OnContextualTutorialRequested?.Invoke(tutorialId);
00400:             EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00401:             return true;
00402:         }
00403:
00404:         /// <summary>Removes one displayed contextual lesson from the persisted queue.</summary>
00405:         public bool AcknowledgeContextualTutorial(string tutorialId)
00406:         {
00407:             if (_state.contextualTutorialQueue == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tutorialId)) return false;
00408:             bool removed = _state.contextualTutorialQueue.Remove(tutorialId);
00409:             if (removed) EmitJourneyChangedIf();
00410:             return removed;
00411:         }
00412:
00413:         public void RecordShowMeWhere(OnboardingStage stage)
00414:         {
00415:             if (!_state.stagesGuided.Contains((int)stage))
00416:                 _state.stagesGuided.Add((int)stage);
00417:         }
00418:
00419:         public bool HasShownShowMeWhere(OnboardingStage stage)
00420:             => _state.stagesGuided.Contains((int)stage);
00421:
00422:         public OnboardingStageDef CurrentStageDef =>
00423:             OnboardingCatalog.DefFor(Profile, CurrentStage);
00424:
00425:         public bool IsStageRequirementsSatisfied(OnboardingStageDef def)
00426:             => AreRequirementsSatisfied(def);
00427:
00428:         public IReadOnlyList<OnboardingStage> OutstandingStages()
00429:         {
00430:             var list = new List<OnboardingStage>();
00431:             if (!JourneyComplete)
00432:             {
00433:                 var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile);
00434:                 for (int i = 0; i < order.Count; i++)
00435:                     if (!_state.completedStages.Contains((int)order[i]))
00436:                         list.Add(order[i]);
00437:             }
00438:             return list;
00439:         }
00440:
00441:         private bool AreRequirementsSatisfied(OnboardingStageDef def)
00442:         {
00443:             foreach (var req in def.Requirements)
00444:             {
00445:                 if (string.Equals(req.Sigil, OnboardingCatalog.DaySentinel, StringComparison.Ordinal))
00446:                 {
00447:                     if (_state.day < req.Threshold) return false;
00448:                     continue;
00449:                 }
00450:                 if (!_counts.TryGetValue(req.Sigil, out int count) || count < req.Threshold)
00451:                     return false;
00452:             }
00453:             return true;
00454:         }
00455:
00456:         private bool _suppressEvents;
00457:
00458:         private bool AdvanceStagesAccountingForNonTerminal()
00459:         {
00460:             bool anyChange = false;
00461:             int guard = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile).Count;
00462:             while (guard-- > 0)
00463:             {
00464:                 OnboardingStage stage = CurrentStage;
00465:                 if (Profile == OnboardingProfile.Legacy && IsTerminalStage(stage)) break;
00466:                 int idx = (int)stage;
00467:                 if (_state.completedStages.Contains(idx)) break;
00468:                 if (!AreRequirementsSatisfied(OnboardingCatalog.DefFor(Profile, stage))) break;
00469:
00470:                 _state.completedStages.Add(idx);
00471:                 if (!_suppressEvents) OnStageAdvanced?.Invoke((OnboardingStage)idx);
00472:                 anyChange = true;
00473:                 _state.currentStage = NextIncompleteIndex();
00474:             }
00475:             if (Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour &&
00476:                 OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile).Count == _state.completedStages.Count &&
00477:                 !JourneyComplete)
00478:             {
00479:                 _state.journeyComplete = true;
00480:             }
00481:             return anyChange;
00482:         }
00483:
00484:         private int NextIncompleteIndex()
00485:         {
00486:             var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile);
00487:             for (int i = 0; i < order.Count; i++)
00488:             {
00489:                 if (!_state.completedStages.Contains((int)order[i]))
00490:                     return (int)order[i];
00491:             }
00492:             return (int)order[order.Count - 1];
00493:         }
00494:
00495:         public OnboardingSaveState CaptureState()
00496:         {
00497:             // Build the persistent sigil list with strictly sorted ordinal-stable
00498:             // ordering so SaveChecksum's recursive walk is deterministic and
00499:             // byte-identical across runs.
00500:             var persistedSigils = _counts
00501:                 .Where(pair => pair.Value > 0)
00502:                 .OrderBy(pair => pair.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal)
00503:                 .Select(pair => new OnboardingSigilRecord { key = pair.Key, count = pair.Value })
00504:                 .ToList();
00505:
00506:             return new OnboardingSaveState
00507:             {
00508:                 schemaVersion = _state.schemaVersion,
00509:                 day = _state.day,
00510:                 profile = _state.profile,
00511:                 sigils = persistedSigils,
00512:                 currentStage = _state.currentStage,
00513:                 completedStages = new List<int>(_state.completedStages),
00514:                 journeyComplete = _state.journeyComplete,
00515:                 assistance = _state.assistance,
00516:                 dismissedHints = new List<string>(_state.dismissedHints ?? new List<string>()),
00517:                 stagesGuided = new List<int>(_state.stagesGuided ?? new List<int>()),
00518:                 contextualTutorialSeenIds = new List<string>(_state.contextualTutorialSeenIds ?? new List<string>()),
00519:                 contextualTutorialQueue = new List<string>(_state.contextualTutorialQueue ?? new List<string>()),
00520:             };
00521:         }
00522:
00523:         public static OnboardingJourney Restore(OnboardingSaveState saved)
00524:         {
00525:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00526:             if (saved == null) return j;
00527:
00528:             int futureVersion = saved.schemaVersion;
00529:             if (futureVersion <= 0)
00530:                 futureVersion = SaveVersion;
00531:             if (futureVersion > SaveVersion)
00532:                 throw new InvalidOperationException(
00533:                     $"Onboarding save version {futureVersion} is not supported by this build (max v{SaveVersion}).");
00534:
00535:             // Reconstruction is idempotent — suppress per-step events so
00536:             // listeners attached after Restore don't see a flood, but emit
00537:             // one journey-changed after Restore completes.
00538:             j._suppressEvents = true;
00539:
00540:             j._state = new OnboardingSaveState
00541:             {
00542:                 schemaVersion = futureVersion,
00543:                 day = saved.day == 0 ? 1 : saved.day,
00544:                 profile = saved.profile == (int)OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00545:                     ? (int)OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00546:                     : (int)OnboardingProfile.Legacy,
00547:                 currentStage = NormaliseStage(
00548:                     saved.profile == (int)OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00549:                         ? OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00550:                         : OnboardingProfile.Legacy,
00551:                     saved.currentStage),
00552:                 completedStages = saved.completedStages != null
00553:                     ? new List<int>(saved.completedStages)
00554:                     : new List<int>(),
00555:                 journeyComplete = saved.journeyComplete,
00556:                 assistance = saved.assistance,
00557:                 dismissedHints = saved.dismissedHints != null
00558:                     ? new List<string>(saved.dismissedHints)
00559:                     : new List<string>(),
00560:                 stagesGuided = saved.stagesGuided != null
00561:                     ? new List<int>(saved.stagesGuided)
00562:                     : new List<int>(),
00563:                 contextualTutorialSeenIds = saved.contextualTutorialSeenIds != null
00564:                     ? new List<string>(saved.contextualTutorialSeenIds)
00565:                     : new List<string>(),
00566:                 contextualTutorialQueue = saved.contextualTutorialQueue != null
00567:                     ? new List<string>(saved.contextualTutorialQueue)
00568:                     : new List<string>(),
00569:             };
00570:
00571:             // Reconstruct runtime counter map from the persisted ordinal list.
00572:             if (saved.sigils != null)
00573:             {
00574:                 for (int i = 0; i < saved.sigils.Count; i++)
00575:                 {
00576:                     var rec = saved.sigils[i];
00577:                     if (rec == null || rec.count <= 0 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(rec.key)) continue;
00578:                     j._counts[rec.key] = rec.count;
00579:                 }
00580:             }
00581:
00582:             // Reconcile the legacy terminal stage from the real day boundary.
00583:             // First-hour completion is signal-driven and must not be fabricated
00584:             // by a day change during load.
00585:             bool dayRealAdvance = j.Profile == OnboardingProfile.Legacy && j._state.day >= 2;
00586:             if (dayRealAdvance && !j._state.completedStages.Contains((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance))
00587:                 j._state.completedStages.Add((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00588:             if (j.Profile == OnboardingProfile.Legacy)
00589:             {
00590:                 j._state.journeyComplete =
00591:                     dayRealAdvance && j._state.completedStages.Contains((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance);
00592:             }
00593:             else
00594:             {
00595:                 // Additive stages (Duty/Dose, 2026-09-25) must never demote a
00596:                 // campaign that already completed the first-hour contract before
00597:                 // they existed; new campaigns complete via the live signal path.
00598:                 j._state.journeyComplete = saved.journeyComplete || OnboardingCatalog.FirstHourOrder
00599:                     .All(stage => j._state.completedStages.Contains((int)stage));
00600:             }
00601:
00602:             // Walk forward over any earlier stages that had already met their
00603:             // requirements at save-time so the resume is exactly correct.
00604:             int guard = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(j.Profile).Count;
00605:             while (guard-- > 0 &&
00606:                    (j.Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour || !j.IsTerminalStage(j.CurrentStage)) &&
00607:                    j.AreRequirementsSatisfied(j.CurrentStageDef))
00608:             {
00609:                 int idx = j._state.currentStage;
00610:                 if (j._state.completedStages.Contains(idx)) break;
00611:                 j._state.completedStages.Add(idx);
00612:                 j._state.currentStage = j.NextIncompleteIndex();
00613:             }
00614:
00615:             j._state.currentStage = j.NextIncompleteIndex();
00616:
00617:             j._suppressEvents = false;
00618:             j.OnJourneyChanged?.Invoke(j);
00619:             return j;
00620:         }
00621:
00622:         private static int NormaliseStage(OnboardingProfile profile, int idx)
00623:         {
00624:             var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(profile);
00625:             for (int i = 0; i < order.Count; i++)
00626:                 if ((int)order[i] == idx) return idx;
00627:             return (int)order[0];
00628:         }
00629:
00630:         private int CurrentPosition()
00631:         {
00632:             var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(Profile);
00633:             for (int i = 0; i < order.Count; i++)
00634:                 if (order[i] == CurrentStage) return i;
00635:             return 0;
00636:         }
00637:
00638:         private bool IsTerminalStage(OnboardingStage stage) =>
00639:             Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00640:                 ? stage == OnboardingStage.Expedition
00641:                 : stage == OnboardingStage.DayAdvance;
00642:
00643:         private void EmitJourneyChangedIf()
00644:         {
00645:             if (_suppressEvents) return;
00646:             OnJourneyChanged?.Invoke(this);
00647:         }
00648:     }
00649: }
```

00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004:
00005: namespace Ashfall.Core.Onboarding
00006: {
00007:     /// <summary>
00008:     /// Ordered stages of the first-hour onboarding journey. Each stage declares
00009:     /// real, observable completion signals ("sigils") emitted by the live host
00010:     /// when the player performs genuine systems actions. Nothing here fabricates
00011:     /// resources or bypasses gameplay rules.
00012:     /// </summary>
00013:     public enum OnboardingStage
00014:     {
00015:         /// <summary>Resolve all three Opening Protocol directives.</summary>
00016:         Protocol = 0,
00017:
00018:         /// <summary>Inspect at least three bunker rooms.</summary>
00019:         Inspect = 1,
00020:
00021:         /// <summary>Set a ration policy and open the stores.</summary>
00022:         Rationing = 2,
00023:
00024:         /// <summary>Assign at least one survivor to a duty.</summary>
00025:         Assignment = 3,
00026:
00027:         /// <summary>Read the weather (forecast, history, or panel).</summary>
00028:         Weather = 4,
00029:
00030:         /// <summary>Equip or consume an item from the real inventory.</summary>
00031:         InventoryUse = 5,
00032:
00033:         /// <summary>Commit the first day advance to reach Day 2.</summary>
00034:         DayAdvance = 6,
00035:
00036:         /// <summary>Start and complete a real water-treatment operation.</summary>
00037:         Water = 7,
00038:
00039:         /// <summary>Operate a real shelter power breaker.</summary>
00040:         Power = 8,
00041:
00042:         /// <summary>Consume a real food ration from the inventory.</summary>
00043:         Food = 9,
00044:
00045:         /// <summary>Start a real research node.</summary>
00046:         Research = 10,
00047:
00048:         /// <summary>Dispatch a real expedition.</summary>
00049:         Expedition = 11,
00050:
00051:         /// <summary>Assign a survivor to a real duty shift. Functional demand: survivors do not work unassigned.</summary>
00052:         Duty = 12,
00053:
00054:         /// <summary>Read the dose ledger or radiation detail. Dose accumulates quietly.</summary>
00055:         Dose = 13
00056:     }
00057:
00058:     /// <summary>
00059:     /// Selects the onboarding contract used by a campaign. Legacy saves retain
00060:     /// the original protocol journey; new campaigns use the state-true
00061:     /// first-hour sequence.
00062:     /// </summary>
00063:     public enum OnboardingProfile
00064:     {
00065:         Legacy = 0,
00066:         FirstHour = 1
00067:     }
00068:
00069:     /// <summary>
00070:     /// Assistance intensity for contextual onboarding hints. Persisted with the
00071:     /// journey so a returning player keeps their chosen level.
00072:     /// </summary>
00073:     public enum OnboardingAssistance
00074:     {
00075:         Minimal = 0,   // Objective text only.
00076:         Standard = 1,  // Objective + contextual hints after inactivity/failure.
00077:         Guided = 2     // Steps are auto-highlighted; hints offer "show me where".
00078:     }
00079:
00080:     /// <summary>
00081:     /// Result of recording one observed signal against the journey.
00082:     /// </summary>
00083:     public enum OnboardingSignalResult
00084:     {
00085:         /// <summary>The signal is meaningless to the journey (ignored).</summary>
00086:         Ignored = 0,
00087:
00088:         /// <summary>Recorded, but the active stage is not yet satisfied.</summary>
00089:         Progressed = 1,
00090:
00091:         /// <summary>Recorded and the active stage became satisfied (journey may advance).</summary>
00092:         Advanced = 2
00093:     }
00094:
00095:     /// <summary>
00096:     /// A stable ordered (signal name, count) pair. Used in
00097:     /// <see cref="OnboardingSaveState.sigils"/> so the save checksum's
00098:     /// recursive walk produces a deterministic serial form irrespective of the
00099:     /// underlying dictionary/enumeration semantics.
00100:     /// </summary>
00101:     [Serializable]
00102:     public sealed class OnboardingSigilRecord
00103:     {
00104:         public string key = string.Empty;
00105:         public int count;
00106:     }
00107:
00108:     /// <summary>
00109:     /// Serialized onboarding progress. Public fields only (SaveChecksum walks
00110:     /// public fields); snake_case for wire-format parity with the other save stores.
00111:     /// </summary>
00112:     [Serializable]
00113:     public sealed class OnboardingSaveState
00114:     {
00115:         public int schemaVersion = OnboardingJourney.SaveVersion;
00116:         public int day = 1;
00117:         public int profile = (int)OnboardingProfile.Legacy;
00118:
00119:         /// <summary>
00120:         /// Ordinal-stable signal list — the checksum recursive walk iterates this
00121:         /// list in documented order rather than relying on a Dictionary's
00122:         /// behavioural enumeration semantics.
00123:         /// </summary>
00124:         public List<OnboardingSigilRecord> sigils = new List<OnboardingSigilRecord>();
00125:
00126:         public int currentStage = (int)OnboardingStage.Protocol;
00127:         public List<int> completedStages = new List<int>();
00128:         public bool journeyComplete;
00129:         public int assistance = (int)OnboardingAssistance.Standard;
00130:
00131:         /// <summary>Dismissed contextual hint keys (persisted so they stay dismissed).</summary>
00132:         public List<string> dismissedHints = new List<string>();
00133:
00134:         /// <summary>One-shot "show me where" guidance already offered per stage.</summary>
00135:         public List<int> stagesGuided = new List<int>();
00136:
00137:         /// <summary>Contextual tutorial IDs shown by domain milestones.</summary>
00138:         public List<string> contextualTutorialSeenIds = new List<string>();
00139:
00140:         /// <summary>Contextual tutorial IDs waiting for presentation.</summary>
00141:         public List<string> contextualTutorialQueue = new List<string>();
00142:     }
00143: }

00116:

00118:         {

00120:             if (KeyBindings != null)

00125:                 }

00134:                 UiScale = UiScale,

00139:                 SfxVolume = SfxVolume,
00140:                 RadioVolume = RadioVolume,
00141:                 AmbienceVolume = AmbienceVolume,
00142:                 MuteAll = MuteAll,
00143:                 Locale = Locale ?? "en",
00144:                 HighContrast = HighContrast,
00145:                 HazardTextLabels = HazardTextLabels,
00146:                 ReducedMotion = ReducedMotion,
00147:                 VisualAudioAlerts = VisualAudioAlerts,
00148:                 AudioMixPreset = AudioMixPreset ?? "preset_full_dynamic",
00149:                 LargeFonts = LargeFonts,
00150:                 ColorblindMode = ColorblindMode ?? ColorblindColorMapper.None,
00151:                 TutorialMode = TutorialMode,
00152:                 ConfirmEndDay = ConfirmEndDay,
00153:                 VerboseRadioLog = VerboseRadioLog,
00154:                 AutoSaveOnDay = AutoSaveOnDay,
00155:                 ModsEnabled = ModsEnabled,
00156:                 EnabledMods = new List<string>(EnabledMods ?? new List<string>()),
00157:                 KeyBindings = clonedBindings
00158:             };
00159:         }
00160:     }
00161: }

## `Assets/Ashfall.Core/Settings/UserSettingsCodec.cs` — 230 lines; 9,327 bytes; SHA-256 `b97839c0248332456b033694ee2a007926d40a595200d020e2d5c933ff4ed39a`

- 00052: public static UserSettingsData Sanitize(UserSettingsData? data, out string? diagnosticMessage)
- 00205: private static float SanitizeVolume(string name, float volume, float defaultVal, List<string> warnings)
- 00224: public static string Serialize(UserSettingsData data)
```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004: using System.Text.Json;
00005:
00006: namespace Ashfall.Core.Settings
00007: {
00008:     /// <summary>
00009:     /// Engine-agnostic codec and recovery validator for UserSettings JSON data.
00010:     /// Guarantees that malformed, corrupted, truncated, or out-of-range user settings
00011:     /// always recover to safe defaults with informative diagnostic logging without throwing.
00012:     /// </summary>
00013:     public static class UserSettingsCodec
00014:     {
00015:         public static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOpts = new()
00016:         {
00017:             WriteIndented = true,
00018:             PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true
00019:         };
00020:
00021:         /// <summary>
00022:         /// Safely deserializes settings JSON with complete recovery fallback.
00023:         /// Never throws; preserves any warning/diagnostic message in the return tuple.
00024:         /// </summary>
00025:         public static (UserSettingsData Data, string? DiagnosticMessage) DeserializeWithRecovery(string? json)
00026:         {
00027:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(json))
00028:             {
00029:                 return (new UserSettingsData(), "[UserSettingsCodec] Settings JSON is empty or whitespace. Recovered with safe defaults.");
00030:             }
00031:
00032:             try
00033:             {
00034:                 var data = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<UserSettingsData>(json, JsonOpts);
00035:                 if (data == null)
00036:                 {
00037:                     return (new UserSettingsData(), "[UserSettingsCodec] Deserialization returned null. Recovered with safe defaults.");
00038:                 }
00039:
00040:                 data = Sanitize(data, out string? sanitizeMsg);
00041:                 return (data, sanitizeMsg);
00042:             }
00043:             catch (Exception ex)
00044:             {
00045:                 return (new UserSettingsData(), $"[UserSettingsCodec] Invalid settings JSON ({ex.Message}). Recovered with safe defaults.");
00046:             }
00047:         }
00048:
00049:         /// <summary>
00050:         /// Sanitizes all settings fields, clamping out-of-range values to valid boundaries.
00051:         /// </summary>
00052:         public static UserSettingsData Sanitize(UserSettingsData? data, out string? diagnosticMessage)
00053:         {
00054:             if (data == null)
00055:             {
00056:                 diagnosticMessage = "[UserSettingsCodec] Null data provided for sanitization. Returning fresh defaults.";
00057:                 return new UserSettingsData();
00058:             }
00059:
00060:             var warnings = new List<string>();
00061:
00062:             // Window Mode (0=Windowed, 1=Borderless, 2=Fullscreen)
00063:             if (data.WindowMode < 0 || data.WindowMode > 2)
00064:             {
00065:                 warnings.Add($"WindowMode {data.WindowMode} clamped to 0 (Windowed)");
00066:                 data.WindowMode = 0;
00067:             }
00068:
00069:             // Resolution
00070:             if (data.ResolutionWidth < 640 || data.ResolutionWidth > 7680)
00071:             {
00072:                 warnings.Add($"ResolutionWidth {data.ResolutionWidth} clamped to 1920");
00073:                 data.ResolutionWidth = 1920;
00074:             }
00075:             if (data.ResolutionHeight < 480 || data.ResolutionHeight > 4320)
00076:             {
00077:                 warnings.Add($"ResolutionHeight {data.ResolutionHeight} clamped to 1080");
00078:                 data.ResolutionHeight = 1080;
00079:             }
00080:
00081:             // UI Scale
00082:             if (float.IsNaN(data.UiScale) || float.IsInfinity(data.UiScale) || data.UiScale < 0.5f || data.UiScale > 2.5f)
00083:             {
00084:                 warnings.Add($"UiScale {data.UiScale} clamped to 1.0");
00085:                 data.UiScale = 1.0f;
00086:             }
00087:
00088:             // Locale
00089:             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(data.Locale))
00090:             {
00091:                 data.Locale = "en";
00092:             }
00093:             else
00094:             {
00095:                 data.Locale = data.Locale.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
00096:                 if (data.Locale != "en" && data.Locale != "de" && data.Locale != "pseudo")
00097:                 {
00098:                     warnings.Add($"Locale '{data.Locale}' not recognized; defaulting to 'en'");
00099:                     data.Locale = "en";
00100:                 }
00101:             }
00102:
00103:             data.EnabledMods ??= new List<string>();
00104:             var sanitizedMods = new List<string>();
00105:             var seenMods = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00106:             foreach (string? modId in data.EnabledMods)
00107:             {
00108:                 string normalized = modId?.Trim().ToLowerInvariant() ?? string.Empty;
00109:                 if (!Ashfall.Core.Mods.JsonModLayering.IsSafeModId(normalized))
00110:                 {
00111:                     if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(modId))
00112:                         warnings.Add($"Enabled mod '{modId}' ignored because its id is not path-safe");
00113:                     continue;
00114:                 }
00115:                 if (seenMods.Add(normalized))
00116:                     sanitizedMods.Add(normalized);
00117:             }
00118:             data.EnabledMods = sanitizedMods;
00119:
00120:             // Colorblind mode (Plan 184)
00121:             string cb = ColorblindColorMapper.NormalizeMode(data.ColorblindMode);
00122:             if (!string.Equals(data.ColorblindMode?.Trim(), cb, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
00123:                 || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(data.ColorblindMode))
00124:             {
00125:                 if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(data.ColorblindMode)
00126:                     && !ColorblindColorMapper.IsKnownMode(data.ColorblindMode))
00127:                 {
00128:                     warnings.Add($"ColorblindMode '{data.ColorblindMode}' not recognized; defaulting to '{ColorblindColorMapper.None}'");
00129:                 }
00130:                 data.ColorblindMode = cb;
00131:             }
00132:             else
00133:             {
00134:                 data.ColorblindMode = cb;
00135:             }
00136:
00137:             // Tutorial Mode (0=All, 1=ContextualOnly, 2=Disabled)
00138:             if (data.TutorialMode < 0 || data.TutorialMode > 2)
00139:             {
00140:                 warnings.Add($"TutorialMode {data.TutorialMode} clamped to 0");
00141:                 data.TutorialMode = 0;
00142:             }
00143:
00144:             // Max FPS (0 is valid for unlimited, otherwise capped at 360)
00145:             if (data.MaxFps < 0 || data.MaxFps > 360)
00146:             {
00147:                 warnings.Add($"MaxFps {data.MaxFps} clamped to 60");
00148:                 data.MaxFps = 60;
00149:             }
00150:
00151:             // Audio Volumes
00152:             data.MasterVolume = SanitizeVolume("MasterVolume", data.MasterVolume, 1.0f, warnings);
00153:             data.MusicVolume = SanitizeVolume("MusicVolume", data.MusicVolume, 0.8f, warnings);
00154:             data.SfxVolume = SanitizeVolume("SfxVolume", data.SfxVolume, 0.8f, warnings);
00155:             data.RadioVolume = SanitizeVolume("RadioVolume", data.RadioVolume, 0.9f, warnings);
00156:             data.AmbienceVolume = SanitizeVolume("AmbienceVolume", data.AmbienceVolume, 0.7f, warnings);
00157:
00158:             // Key Bindings (Plan 37)
00159:             if (data.KeyBindings == null)
00160:             {
00161:                 data.KeyBindings = new Dictionary<string, List<int>>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00162:             }
00163:             else
00164:             {
00165:                 var sanitizedBindings = new Dictionary<string, List<int>>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00166:                 foreach (var (action, keyList) in data.KeyBindings)
00167:                 {
00168:                     if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(action))
00169:                     {
00170:                         warnings.Add("Ignored null or whitespace keybinding action");
00171:                         continue;
00172:                     }
00173:
00174:                     if (keyList == null)
00175:                     {
00176:                         warnings.Add($"Null key list for action '{action}' reset to empty");
00177:                         sanitizedBindings[action] = new List<int>();
00178:                         continue;
00179:                     }
00180:
00181:                     var validKeys = new List<int>();
00182:                     foreach (int code in keyList)
00183:                     {
00184:                         if (code > 0 && code <= 0x01FFFFFF)
00185:                         {
00186:                             validKeys.Add(code);
00187:                         }
00188:                         else
00189:                         {
00190:                             warnings.Add($"Invalid keycode {code} for action '{action}' dropped");
00191:                         }
00192:                     }
00193:                     sanitizedBindings[action] = validKeys;
00194:                 }
00195:                 data.KeyBindings = sanitizedBindings;
00196:             }
00197:
00198:             diagnosticMessage = warnings.Count > 0
00199:                 ? "[UserSettingsCodec] Sanitized settings: " + string.Join("; ", warnings)
00200:                 : null;
00201:
00202:             return data;
00203:         }
00204:
00205:         private static float SanitizeVolume(string name, float volume, float defaultVal, List<string> warnings)
00206:         {
00207:             if (float.IsNaN(volume) || float.IsInfinity(volume))
00208:             {
00209:                 warnings.Add($"{name} (non-finite) reset to {defaultVal:0.0}");
00210:                 return defaultVal;
00211:             }
00212:             if (volume < 0.0f || volume > 1.0f)
00213:             {
00214:                 float clamped = Math.Clamp(volume, 0.0f, 1.0f);
00215:                 warnings.Add($"{name} {volume} clamped to {clamped:0.0}");
00216:                 return clamped;
00217:             }
00218:             return volume;
00219:         }
00220:
00221:         /// <summary>
00222:         /// Serializes settings data to clean formatted JSON after sanitization.
00223:         /// </summary>
00224:         public static string Serialize(UserSettingsData data)
00225:         {
00226:             var sanitized = Sanitize(data, out _);
00227:             return JsonSerializer.Serialize(sanitized, JsonOpts);
00228:         }
00229:     }
00230: }
```

00104:             try

00109:
