00219:         public static bool GetJournalTabNumber(InputEvent @event, out int tab)
00220:         {
00221:             if (@event.IsActionPressed(JournalTab1)) { tab = 1; return true; }
00222:             if (@event.IsActionPressed(JournalTab2)) { tab = 2; return true; }
00223:             if (@event.IsActionPressed(JournalTab3)) { tab = 3; return true; }
00224:             if (@event.IsActionPressed(JournalTab4)) { tab = 4; return true; }
00225:             if (@event.IsActionPressed(JournalTab5)) { tab = 5; return true; }
00226:             tab = 0;
00227:             return false;
00228:         }
00229:
00230:         /// <summary>
00231:         /// Returns the human-readable prompt string for an action (e.g. "[J]" or "[F1]")
00232:         /// derived dynamically from the runtime InputMap rather than hardcoding keys in tutorial copy.
00233:         /// </summary>
00234:         public static string GetActionPrompt(string action)
00235:         {
00236:             if (InputMap.HasAction(action))
00237:             {
00238:                 var events = InputMap.ActionGetEvents(action);
00239:                 foreach (var ev in events)
00240:                 {
00241:                     if (ev is InputEventKey key)
00242:                     {
00243:                         string keyStr = OS.GetKeycodeString(key.PhysicalKeycode != Key.None ? key.PhysicalKeycode : key.Keycode);
00244:                         if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(keyStr))
00245:                             return $"[{keyStr}]";
00246:                     }
00247:                 }
00248:             }
00249:
00250:             // Fallback default prompts if headless / uninitialized
00251:             return action switch
00252:             {
00253:                 Close => "[Esc]",
00254:                 Confirm => "[Enter]",
00255:                 NextTab => "[Tab]",
00256:                 Journal => "[J]",
00257:                 Help => "[F1]",
00258:                 Forecast => "[F]",
00259:                 WeatherHistory => "[H]",
00260:                 Events => "[E]",
00261:                 Expeditions => "[X]",
00262:                 Holdfast => "[T]",
00263:                 _ => $"[{action}]"
00264:             };
00265:         }
00266:     }
00267: }

## `src/Host/OnboardingSaveStore.cs` — 49 lines; 2,269 bytes; SHA-256 `2a24a336297dd2c6efdfae5f5eb4162ec159a81a019d827719824a69176798f9`
Declaration index:
- 00026: public static class OnboardingSaveStore
- 00036: public static bool TrySave(OnboardingSaveState state, string? pathOverride = null)
- 00039: public static OnboardingSaveState? TryLoad(string? pathOverride = null)
- 00046: public static string TryCapturePersisted(OnboardingSaveState state)
```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // ============================================================================
00003: // Save Store : OnboardingSaveStore
00004: // Core State : Ashfall.Core.Onboarding.OnboardingSaveState
00005: // Host Caller: Main.Onboarding / OnboardingHostSession
00006: // Purpose    : First-hour onboarding journey progress (stage, dismissed
00007: //              hints, assistance level, completion) — survives both
00008: //              game-shutdown and reset, so the player resumes the journey
00009: //              at the correct step after save/load.
00010: // ============================================================================
00011: using System;
00012: using Ashfall.Core;
00013: using Ashfall.Core.Onboarding;
00014: using Ashfall.Core.Save;
00015:
00016: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp
00017: {
00018:     /// <summary>
00019:     /// Persists <see cref="OnboardingSaveState"/> as checksummed JSON under
00020:     /// <c>user://onboarding_save.json</c>. Thin façade over the Core
00021:     /// <c>SaveStore&lt;T&gt;</c> service (via <see cref="SaveStoreHub.Checksummed{T}"/>):
00022:     /// pattern, path resolution, atomic write, and error handling live in the
00023:     /// service. The legacy bare-state fallback is disabled (no pre-checksum
00024:     /// format existed for this section).
00025:     /// </summary>
00026:     public static class OnboardingSaveStore
00027:     {
00028:         public const string FileName = "onboarding_save.json";
00029:         public const string SectionName = "onboarding";
00030:
00031:         private static readonly SaveStore<OnboardingSaveState> s_store = SaveStoreHub.Checksummed<OnboardingSaveState>(
00032:             FileName, nameof(OnboardingSaveStore), createBackup: false, allowLegacyBareState: false);
00033:
00034:         public static string SavePath => s_store.SavePath;
00035:
00036:         public static bool TrySave(OnboardingSaveState state, string? pathOverride = null)
00037:             => s_store.TrySave(state, pathOverride);
00038:
00039:         public static OnboardingSaveState? TryLoad(string? pathOverride = null)
00040:             => s_store.TryLoad(pathOverride);
00041:
00042:         /// <summary>
00043:         /// Capture the exact persisted bytes for the campaign envelope without
00044:         /// writing to disk (preserves byte shape used by <c>SaveAll</c>).
00045:         /// </summary>
00046:         public static string TryCapturePersisted(OnboardingSaveState state)
00047:             => s_store.CapturePersisted(state);
00048:     }
00049: }
```

00068:             "Power — Air and water filters need watts. A brownout stops filtration and indoor radiation climbs.",

00215:                 _assistanceLabel.Text = T("onboarding.assistance.standard", "ASSISTANCE: STANDARD");
00216:                 EmptyChecklist();
00217:                 SetActionEnabledStates(false);
00218:                 UpdateAssistanceButtonLabel(OnboardingAssistance.Standard);
00219:                 return;
00220:             }
00221:

00223:             bool complete = j.JourneyComplete;
00224:

00227:                 : string.Format(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
00228:                     T("onboarding.status.current", "CURRENT: {0}"),
00229:                     LocalizedStageTitle(def));
00230:             _objectiveLabel.Text = complete
00231:                 ? T(j.Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00232:                     ? "onboarding.status.first_hour_complete"
00233:                     : "onboarding.status.day_two",
00234:                     j.Profile == OnboardingProfile.FirstHour
00235:                         ? "The first-hour sequence is complete. The shelter is yours to manage."
00236:                         : "You reached Day 2. Returning the ledger to your command.")
00237:                 : LocalizedStageObjective(def);
00238:             _hintLabel.Text = BuildHintLine(j, def);
00239:             _assistanceLabel.Text = AssistanceLabel(j.Assistance);
00240:
00241:             RebuildChecklist(j);
00242:             SetActionEnabledStates(!complete);
00243:             UpdateAssistanceButtonLabel(j.Assistance);
00244:         }

00321:         {
00322:             if (_checklist == null) return;
00323:             AshfallUiHelpers.EmptyChildren(_checklist);

00325:                 "Onboarding is not active for this run.")));

00327:
00328:         private void RebuildChecklist(OnboardingJourney j)
00329:         {
00330:             if (_checklist == null) return;
00331:             AshfallUiHelpers.EmptyChildren(_checklist);
00332:
00333:             var order = OnboardingCatalog.OrderFor(j.Profile);
00334:             for (int i = 0; i < order.Count; i++)
00335:             {
00336:                 var def = OnboardingCatalog.DefFor(j.Profile, order[i]);
00337:                 bool isCurrent = (int)def.Id == (int)j.CurrentStage && !j.JourneyComplete;
00338:                 bool done = j.IsStageComplete(def.Id);
00339:                 string glyph = done ? "[OK]" : (isCurrent ? "[..]" : "[ ]");
00340:                 var lbl = new Label
00341:                 {
00342:                     Text = $"  {glyph}  {LocalizedStageTitle(def)}",
00343:                 };
00344:                 lbl.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.FontSizeBody);
00345:                 var colorToken = done
00346:                     ? Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.Muted
00347:                     : (isCurrent ? Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.Hot : Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.Pale);
00348:                 lbl.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color",
00349:                     AshfallUiHelpers.ToColor(colorToken));
00350:                 _checklist.AddChild(lbl);
00351:             }
00352:         }
00353:
00354:         private static string BuildHintLine(OnboardingJourney j, OnboardingStageDef def)
00355:         {
00356:             string contextualKey = def.Id switch
00357:             {
00358:                 OnboardingStage.Protocol => "onboarding.hint.protocol",
00359:                 OnboardingStage.Inspect => "onboarding.hint.inspect",
00360:                 OnboardingStage.Rationing => "onboarding.hint.rationing",
00361:                 OnboardingStage.Assignment => "onboarding.hint.assignment",
00362:                 OnboardingStage.Weather => "onboarding.hint.weather",
00363:                 OnboardingStage.InventoryUse => "onboarding.hint.inventory",
00364:                 OnboardingStage.DayAdvance => "onboarding.hint.day_advance",
00365:                 OnboardingStage.Water => "onboarding.hint.water",
00366:                 OnboardingStage.Power => "onboarding.hint.power",
00367:                 OnboardingStage.Food => "onboarding.hint.food",
00368:                 OnboardingStage.Research => "onboarding.hint.research",
00369:                 OnboardingStage.Expedition => "onboarding.hint.expedition",
00370:                 _ => "onboarding.hint.empty",
00371:             };
00372:             string fallback = def.Id switch
00373:             {
00374:                 OnboardingStage.Protocol => "Pick ration, maintenance, and radio. The bunker stores adjust around your choices.",
00375:                 OnboardingStage.Inspect => "Inspect any three rooms — every confirming note is a fallback if the next storm cuts light.",
00376:                 OnboardingStage.Rationing => "Open the stores. Read the food and water you are rationing against.",
00377:                 OnboardingStage.Assignment => "Pull one survivor onto a duty from the Duty Roster. Their shifts move the bunker forward.",
00378:                 OnboardingStage.Weather => "Read the forecast before you end the day — fallout storms change outdoor rad.",
00379:                 OnboardingStage.InventoryUse => "Equip something real. The geiger or gas mask only protects the hands that wear them.",
00380:                 OnboardingStage.DayAdvance => "Confirm the advance. The morning briefing returns once Day 2 lands.",
00381:                 OnboardingStage.Water => "Start a treatment batch at the water plant. The stores only change if a batch actually runs.",
00382:                 OnboardingStage.Power => "Open the grid and throw one breaker. Watch the rooms change.",
00383:                 OnboardingStage.Food => "Eat one ration from stores. The count should drop.",
00384:                 OnboardingStage.Research => "Start an available research node. It appears in the queue.",
00385:                 OnboardingStage.Expedition => "Send a team with the expedition command. They leave the shelter.",
00386:                 _ => "HINT: —",
00387:             };
00388:
00389:             string hintKey = MakeHintKey(def.Id);
00390:             bool dismissed = j.IsHintDismissed(hintKey);
00391:             if (dismissed) return T("onboarding.hint.dismissed", "HINT: (dismissed)");
00392:
00393:             if (j.Assistance == OnboardingAssistance.Minimal)
00394:                 return string.Empty;
00395:             return $"{T("onboarding.hint.prefix", "HINT:")} {T(contextualKey, fallback)}";
00396:         }
00397:
00398:         private static string T(string key, string fallback) =>
00399:             AshfallLocalization.Tr(key, fallback);
00400:
00401:         private static string LocalizedStageTitle(OnboardingStageDef def) =>
00402:             T(StageKey(def.Id, "title"), def.Title).ToUpperInvariant();
00403:
00404:         private static string LocalizedStageObjective(OnboardingStageDef def) =>
00405:             T(StageKey(def.Id, "objective"), def.Objective);
00406:
00407:         private static string StageKey(OnboardingStage id, string part) =>
00408:             $"onboarding.{id switch
00409:             {
00410:                 OnboardingStage.InventoryUse => "inventory_use",
00411:                 OnboardingStage.DayAdvance => "day_advance",
00412:                 _ => id.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()
00413:             }}.{part}";
00414:
00415:         private static string AssistanceLabel(OnboardingAssistance assistance) =>
00416:             assistance switch
00417:             {
00418:                 OnboardingAssistance.Minimal => T("onboarding.assistance.minimal", "ASSISTANCE: MINIMAL"),
00419:                 OnboardingAssistance.Guided => T("onboarding.assistance.guided", "ASSISTANCE: GUIDED"),
00420:                 _ => T("onboarding.assistance.standard", "ASSISTANCE: STANDARD"),
00421:             };
00422:
00423:         public static string MakeHintKey(OnboardingStage id) => $"stage.{(int)id}.hint";
00424:     }
00425: }

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004: using Godot;
00005:
00006: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI
00007: {
00008:     /// <summary>
00009:     /// ASHFALL — Spatial UI Focus Navigation (Plan 37 / Continuity Wave 5).
00010:     /// Directs keyboard nav keys (arrow keys) and controller D-pad / stick input
00011:     /// to move focus spatially across focusable controls, enforcing grid consistency.
00012:     /// </summary>
00013:     public static class AshfallFocusNavigator
00014:     {
00015:         private static double _stickRepeatCooldown;
00016:         private const double StickRepeatCadenceSeconds = 0.15;
00017:
00018:         /// <summary>
00019:         /// Move focus within root in the given direction.
00020:         /// Finds the nearest focusable whose center lies in the requested half-plane,
00021:         /// preferring same-row/column band (grid rule).
00022:         /// Returns true if focus was successfully moved.
00023:         /// </summary>
00024:         public static bool MoveDirection(Control root, Vector2I direction)
00025:         {
00026:             if (root == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(root) || !root.Visible)
00027:                 return false;
00028:
00029:             var focusables = AshfallFocusPolicy.FindFocusableControls(root);
00030:             if (focusables.Count == 0)
00031:                 return false;
00032:
00033:             var currentFocus = root.GetViewport()?.GuiGetFocusOwner();
00034:             if (currentFocus == null || !focusables.Contains(currentFocus))
00035:             {
00036:                 focusables[0].GrabFocus();
00037:                 return true;
00038:             }
00039:
00040:             var currentRect = currentFocus.GetGlobalRect();
00041:             var currentCenter = currentRect.GetCenter();
00042:
00043:             Control? bestCandidate = null;
00044:             float bestScore = float.MaxValue;
00045:
00046:             foreach (var candidate in focusables)
00047:             {
00048:                 if (candidate == currentFocus) continue;
00049:                 var candRect = candidate.GetGlobalRect();
00050:                 var candCenter = candRect.GetCenter();
00051:                 var delta = candCenter - currentCenter;
00052:
00053:                 // Check direction half-plane
00054:                 if (direction.X > 0 && delta.X <= 0) continue; // Right
00055:                 if (direction.X < 0 && delta.X >= 0) continue; // Left
00056:                 if (direction.Y > 0 && delta.Y <= 0) continue; // Down
00057:                 if (direction.Y < 0 && delta.Y >= 0) continue; // Up
00058:
00059:                 float primaryDist = MathF.Abs(direction.X != 0 ? delta.X : delta.Y);
00060:                 float secondaryDist = MathF.Abs(direction.X != 0 ? delta.Y : delta.X);
00061:
00062:                 // Grid rule: prefer candidates overlapping in perpendicular band
00063:                 bool perpendicularOverlap = direction.X != 0
00064:                     ? (candRect.Position.Y < currentRect.End.Y && candRect.End.Y > currentRect.Position.Y)
00065:                     : (candRect.Position.X < currentRect.End.X && candRect.End.X > currentRect.Position.X);
00066:
00067:                 float score = primaryDist + (perpendicularOverlap ? secondaryDist * 1.5f : secondaryDist * 5.0f);
00068:
00069:                 if (score < bestScore)
00070:                 {
00071:                     bestScore = score;
00072:                     bestCandidate = candidate;
00073:                 }
00074:             }
00075:
00076:             if (bestCandidate != null)
00077:             {
00078:                 bestCandidate.GrabFocus();
00079:                 return true;
00080:             }
00081:
00082:             return false;
00083:         }
00084:
00085:         /// <summary>
00086:         /// Routes the directional navigation actions (ashfall_nav_up/down/left/right)
00087:         /// to MoveDirection within the active focus scope root.
00088:         /// Returns true when an action was handled.
00089:         /// </summary>
00090:         public static bool HandleNavInput(Control scopeRoot, InputEvent @event)
00091:         {
00092:             if (scopeRoot == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(scopeRoot) || !scopeRoot.Visible)
00093:                 return false;
00094:
00095:             if (AshfallInputActions.IsNavUp(@event))
00096:                 return MoveDirection(scopeRoot, new Vector2I(0, -1));
00097:             if (AshfallInputActions.IsNavDown(@event))
00098:                 return MoveDirection(scopeRoot, new Vector2I(0, 1));
00099:             if (AshfallInputActions.IsNavLeft(@event))
00100:                 return MoveDirection(scopeRoot, new Vector2I(-1, 0));
00101:             if (AshfallInputActions.IsNavRight(@event))
00102:                 return MoveDirection(scopeRoot, new Vector2I(1, 0));
00103:
00104:             return false;
00105:         }
00106:
00107:         /// <summary>
00108:         /// Converts held stick/dpad motion into repeated directional steps on a 150ms cadence.
00109:         /// </summary>
00110:         public static void TickStickRepeat(Control scopeRoot, double delta)
00111:         {
00112:             if (scopeRoot == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(scopeRoot) || !scopeRoot.Visible)
00113:                 return;
00114:
00115:             _stickRepeatCooldown -= delta;
00116:             if (_stickRepeatCooldown > 0)
00117:                 return;
00118:
00119:             Vector2I dir = Vector2I.Zero;
00120:             if (Input.IsActionPressed(AshfallInputActions.NavUp)) dir.Y -= 1;
00121:             if (Input.IsActionPressed(AshfallInputActions.NavDown)) dir.Y += 1;
00122:             if (Input.IsActionPressed(AshfallInputActions.NavLeft)) dir.X -= 1;
00123:             if (Input.IsActionPressed(AshfallInputActions.NavRight)) dir.X += 1;
00124:
00125:             if (dir != Vector2I.Zero)
00126:             {
00127:                 MoveDirection(scopeRoot, dir);
00128:                 _stickRepeatCooldown = StickRepeatCadenceSeconds;
00129:             }
00130:         }
00131:     }
00132: }
```

- 00134: public static bool TrapFocus(Control root, InputEvent @event)

- 00186: public static void RestoreFocusFromRoot(Control root)
```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004: using Godot;
00005: using Ashfall.Core.UI;
00006: using DesignTheme = Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme;
00007:
00008: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI
00009: {
00010:     /// <summary>
00011:     /// ASHFALL — Centralized UI Focus Policy (Plan 80 / Task B21).
00012:     /// Provides deterministic initial focus, modal focus trapping, visible focus styling,
00013:     /// and focus restoration on overlay dismissal.
00014:     /// </summary>
00015:     public static class AshfallFocusPolicy
00016:     {
00017:         private static StyleBoxFlat? _cachedFocusBox;
00018:
00019:         /// <summary>
00020:         /// Creates or returns the canonical high-contrast focus indicator stylebox
00021:         /// with a 2px Hot Amber border and brutalist sharp corners.
00022:         /// </summary>
00023:         public static StyleBoxFlat MakeFocusVisibleStyleBox()
00024:         {
00025:             if (_cachedFocusBox != null)
00026:                 return _cachedFocusBox;
00027:
00028:             _cachedFocusBox = new StyleBoxFlat
00029:             {
00030:                 BgColor = new Color(DesignTheme.Hot.r, DesignTheme.Hot.g, DesignTheme.Hot.b, 0.08f),
00031:                 BorderColor = AshfallUiHelpers.ToColor(DesignTheme.Hot),
00032:                 DrawCenter = true,
00033:             };
00034:             _cachedFocusBox.SetBorderWidthAll(2);
00035:             _cachedFocusBox.SetCornerRadiusAll(0);
00036:             return _cachedFocusBox;
00037:         }
00038:
00039:         /// <summary>
00040:         /// Applies the visible focus style and sets FocusMode to All on the given control.
00041:         /// </summary>
00042:         public static void ApplyFocusVisibleStyle(Control control)
00043:         {
00044:             if (control == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(control))
00045:                 return;
00046:
00047:             control.FocusMode = Control.FocusModeEnum.All;
00048:             var sb = MakeFocusVisibleStyleBox();
00049:             control.AddThemeStyleboxOverride("focus", sb);
00050:         }
00051:
00052:         /// <summary>
00053:         /// Gathers all active, visible focusable controls within the given root container in tree order.
00054:         /// </summary>
00055:         public static List<Control> FindFocusableControls(Control root)
00056:         {
00057:             var result = new List<Control>();
00058:             if (root == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(root))
00059:                 return result;
00060:
00061:             CollectFocusablesRecursive(root, result);
00062:             return result;
00063:         }
00064:
00065:         private static void CollectFocusablesRecursive(Control current, List<Control> result)
00066:         {
00067:             if (current == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(current) || !current.Visible)
00068:                 return;
00069:
00070:             if (current.FocusMode != Control.FocusModeEnum.None &&
00071:                 (current is Button || current is LineEdit || current is TextEdit ||
00072:                  current is OptionButton || current is CheckButton || current is ItemList ||
00073:                  current is AshfallDataGrid || current is Slider))
00074:             {
00075:                 if (current is Button btn && btn.Disabled)
00076:                 {
00077:                     // Disabled buttons don't receive focus navigation
00078:                 }
00079:                 else
00080:                 {
00081:                     result.Add(current);
00082:                 }
00083:             }
00084:
00085:             foreach (var child in current.GetChildren())
00086:             {
00087:                 if (child is Control ctrlChild)
00088:                 {
00089:                     CollectFocusablesRecursive(ctrlChild, result);
00090:                 }
00091:             }
00092:         }
00093:
00094:         /// <summary>
00095:         /// Finds the first interactive focusable child control within root.
00096:         /// </summary>
00097:         public static Control? FindFirstFocusable(Control root)
00098:         {
00099:             var list = FindFocusableControls(root);
00100:             return list.Count > 0 ? list[0] : null;
00101:         }
00102:
00103:         /// <summary>
00104:         /// Deterministically focuses the overlay upon opening.
00105:         /// If initial is provided and valid, it is focused. Otherwise, the first focusable control is focused.
00106:         /// Records opener on root for subsequent restoration.
00107:         /// </summary>
00108:         public static void OpenWithFocus(Control root, Control? initial = null, Control? opener = null)
00109:         {
00110:             if (root == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(root))
00111:                 return;
00112:
00113:             if (opener != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(opener))
00114:             {
00115:                 root.SetMeta("_ashfall_focus_opener", opener);
00116:             }
00117:
00118:             Control? target = initial;
00119:             if (target == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(target) || !target.Visible || !target.IsInsideTree())
00120:             {
00121:                 target = FindFirstFocusable(root);
00122:             }
00123:
00124:             if (target != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(target) && target.IsInsideTree())
00125:             {
00126:                 target.CallDeferred(Control.MethodName.GrabFocus);
00127:             }
00128:         }
00129:
00130:         /// <summary>
00131:         /// Traps keyboard focus cycling (Tab / Shift+Tab) inside root so focus cannot leak to background panels.
00132:         /// Returns true if the event was trapped and handled.
00133:         /// </summary>
00134:         public static bool TrapFocus(Control root, InputEvent @event)
00135:         {
00136:             if (root == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(root) || !root.Visible)
00137:                 return false;
00138:
00139:             if (@event is InputEventKey key && key.Pressed && !key.Echo)
00140:             {
00141:                 if (key.Keycode == Key.Tab)
00142:                 {
00143:                     var focusables = FindFocusableControls(root);
00144:                     if (focusables.Count == 0)
00145:                         return false;
00146:
00147:                     var currentFocus = root.GetViewport()?.GuiGetFocusOwner();
00148:                     int currentIndex = currentFocus != null ? focusables.IndexOf(currentFocus) : -1;
00149:
00150:                     if (key.ShiftPressed)
00151:                     {
00152:                         // Shift+Tab: backwards cycle
00153:                         int prevIndex = currentIndex <= 0 ? focusables.Count - 1 : currentIndex - 1;
00154:                         focusables[prevIndex].GrabFocus();
00155:                         root.GetViewport()?.SetInputAsHandled();
00156:                         return true;
00157:                     }
00158:                     else
00159:                     {
00160:                         // Tab: forward cycle
00161:                         int nextIndex = currentIndex < 0 || currentIndex >= focusables.Count - 1 ? 0 : currentIndex + 1;
00162:                         focusables[nextIndex].GrabFocus();
00163:                         root.GetViewport()?.SetInputAsHandled();
00164:                         return true;
00165:                     }
00166:                 }
00167:             }
00168:
00169:             return false;
00170:         }
00171:
00172:         /// <summary>
00173:         /// Restores focus to the opener control that initiated this modal or panel.
00174:         /// </summary>
00175:         public static void RestoreFocus(Control? opener)
00176:         {
00177:             if (opener != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(opener) && opener.IsInsideTree() && opener.Visible)
00178:             {
00179:                 opener.CallDeferred(Control.MethodName.GrabFocus);
00180:             }
00181:         }
00182:
00183:         /// <summary>
00184:         /// Restores focus using the recorded opener metadata on root, if present.
00185:         /// </summary>
00186:         public static void RestoreFocusFromRoot(Control root)
00187:         {
00188:             if (root == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(root))
00189:                 return;
00190:
00191:             if (root.HasMeta("_ashfall_focus_opener"))
00192:             {
00193:                 var val = root.GetMeta("_ashfall_focus_opener");
00194:                 if (val.VariantType == Variant.Type.Object)
00195:                 {
00196:                     var opener = val.As<Control>();
00197:                     RestoreFocus(opener);
00198:                 }
00199:             }
00200:         }
00201:     }
00202: }
```

## `src/UI/ModalManager.cs` — 134 lines; 4,636 bytes; SHA-256 `ec3ebb135d1456b6d1ef6b4f6f38c1b092e173789df81a3989f51c0c6ece2024`

- 00121: private static void RestoreFocus(Control? target)
- 00129: public static Control? FindFirstFocusable(Control parent)
```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using Godot;
00003: using System;
00004: using Ashfall.Core.UI;
00005:
00006: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI
00007: {
00008:     /// <summary>
00009:     /// Host adapter over Core ModalStackController managing stacked modal panels,
00010:     /// modal input trapping, Escape/Close action handling, and prior keyboard-focus restoration.
00011:     /// </summary>
00012:     public sealed class ModalManager
00013:     {
00014:         private readonly ModalStackController<IModalPanel, Control> _core = new ModalStackController<IModalPanel, Control>();
00015:
00016:         public event Action<IModalPanel>? ModalOpened;
00017:         public event Action<IModalPanel>? ModalClosed;
00018:
00019:         public bool HasActiveModals => _core.HasActiveModals;
00020:         public int ActiveModalCount => _core.ActiveModalCount;
00021:         public IModalPanel? TopModal => _core.TopModal;
00022:
00023:         public ModalManager()
00024:         {
00025:             _core.ModalOpened += modal => ModalOpened?.Invoke(modal);
00026:             _core.ModalClosed += (modal, priorFocus) =>
00027:             {
00028:                 RestoreFocus(priorFocus);
00029:                 ModalClosed?.Invoke(modal);
00030:             };
00031:         }
00032:
00033:         /// <summary>
00034:         /// Pushes and opens a modal panel onto the stack, capturing the current focus owner for restoration.
00035:         /// </summary>
00036:         public void PushModal(IModalPanel modal, Control? priorFocus = null, Control? initialFocus = null)
00037:         {
00038:             if (modal == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(modal));
00039:
00040:             // Capture prior focus owner if not provided
00041:             if (priorFocus == null && modal is Control controlNode && controlNode.IsInsideTree())
00042:             {
00043:                 priorFocus = controlNode.GetViewport()?.GuiGetFocusOwner();
00044:             }
00045:
00046:             _core.PushModal(modal, priorFocus);
00047:             FocusModalControl(modal, initialFocus);
00048:         }
00049:
00050:         /// <summary>
00051:         /// Closes and pops the topmost modal on the stack, restoring focus to the prior control.
00052:         /// </summary>
00053:         public bool PopTopModal()
00054:         {
00055:             return _core.PopTopModal(out _);
00056:         }
00057:
00058:         /// <summary>
00059:         /// Closes all active modals from top to bottom, restoring the initial base focus.
00060:         /// </summary>
00061:         public void CloseAll()
00062:         {
00063:             _core.CloseAll();
00064:         }
00065:
00066:         /// <summary>
00067:         /// Handles unhandled input events for active modals.
00068:         /// Traps Tab cycling, navigates via arrow/D-pad, and handles Escape/Close.
00069:         /// </summary>
00070:         public bool HandleInput(InputEvent @event)
00071:         {
00072:             if (!HasActiveModals || @event == null) return false;
00073:
00074:             var top = TopModal;
00075:             if (top is Control ctrl && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(ctrl) && ctrl.Visible)
00076:             {
00077:                 // 1. Tab trapping inside top modal
00078:                 if (AshfallFocusPolicy.TrapFocus(ctrl, @event))
00079:                 {
00080:                     return true;
00081:                 }
00082:
00083:                 // 2. Nav input within top modal
00084:                 if (AshfallFocusNavigator.HandleNavInput(ctrl, @event))
00085:                 {
00086:                     return true;
00087:                 }
00088:             }
00089:
00090:             // 3. Close on Escape / Cancel
00091:             if (AshfallInputActions.IsCloseOrCancel(@event))
00092:             {
00093:                 if (PopTopModal())
00094:                 {
00095:                     return true;
00096:                 }
00097:             }
00098:
00099:             return false;
00100:         }
00101:
00102:         private static void FocusModalControl(IModalPanel modal, Control? preferredFocus)
00103:         {
00104:             Control? target = preferredFocus ?? modal.InitialFocusControl;
00105:             if (target != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(target) && target.IsInsideTree() && target.Visible)
00106:             {
00107:                 target.CallDeferred(Control.MethodName.GrabFocus);
00108:                 return;
00109:             }
00110:
00111:             if (modal is Control ctrl && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(ctrl) && ctrl.IsInsideTree())
00112:             {
00113:                 var firstInteractive = AshfallFocusPolicy.FindFirstFocusable(ctrl);
00114:                 if (firstInteractive != null)
00115:                 {
00116:                     firstInteractive.CallDeferred(Control.MethodName.GrabFocus);
00117:                 }
00118:             }
00119:         }
00120:
00121:         private static void RestoreFocus(Control? target)
00122:         {
00123:             if (target != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(target) && target.IsInsideTree() && target.Visible)
00124:             {
00125:                 target.CallDeferred(Control.MethodName.GrabFocus);
00126:             }
00127:         }
00128:
00129:         public static Control? FindFirstFocusable(Control parent)
00130:         {
00131:             return AshfallFocusPolicy.FindFirstFocusable(parent);
00132:         }
00133:     }
00134: }
```

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;
00003: using System.Collections.Generic;
00004: using Godot;
00005: using Ashfall.Core;
00006: using Ashfall.Core.Inventory;
00007: using Ashfall.Core.Onboarding;
00008: using AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI;
00009: using AtomicWar.GodotApp.Localization;
00010:
00011: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp
00012: {
00013:     /// <summary>
00014:     /// Main-partial orchestration of the persisted first-hour onboarding
00015:     /// journey. Owns the active <see cref="OnboardingJourney"/>, the
00016:     /// accessible <see cref="OnboardingHintPanel"/>, and the bridge between
00017:     /// genuine runtime commands (panel opens, duty assignments, day advances,
00018:     /// ration directives) and journey signal recording. Implements the spec
00019:     /// substeps:
00020:     /// </summary>
00021:     public partial class Main : Control
00022:     {
00023:         // ── Fields ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
00024:         // The [journey] remains engine-free in Core and survives save/load.
00025:         // Failed actions are tracked per scenario for the contextual-hint
00026:         // heuristic invoked by the hint panel; persisted with the journey
00027:         // are stage / assistance / dismiss / completion.
00028:         private OnboardingJourney? _onboardingJourney;
00029:         private bool _onboardingDirty;
00030:         private int _onboardingFailedActions;
00031:         private double _onboardingLastInteractionSeconds;
00032:         private OnboardingHintPanel? _onboardingHintPanel;
00033:
00034:         // ── Setup / Save / Restore (substeps 5 + 11) ────────────────────
00035:
00036:         private void SetupOnboarding()
00037:         {
00038:             if (_onboardingJourney != null) return;
00039:
00040:             if (AtomicWar.GodotApp.Settings.UserSettingsStore.Current.TutorialMode == 2)
00041:             {
00042:                 _onboardingJourney = null;
00043:                 return;
00044:             }
00045:
00046:             _onboardingJourney = OnboardingJourney.CreateFirstHour();
00047:             try
00048:             {
00049:                 var saved = OnboardingSaveStore.TryLoad();
00050:                 if (saved != null)
00051:                 {
00052:                     _onboardingJourney = OnboardingJourney.Restore(saved);
00053:                 }
00054:             }
00055:             catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
00056:             {
00057:                 GD.PushWarning($"[Onboarding] Save load rejected: {ex.Message}. Starting fresh.");
00058:                 _onboardingJourney = OnboardingJourney.CreateFirstHour();
00059:             }
00060:
00061:             EnsureOnboardingPanel();
00062:             _onboardingHintPanel?.Bind(_onboardingJourney);
00063:             if (_tutorialPanel != null)
00064:             {
00065:                 _tutorialPanel.OnContextualAcknowledged -= OnContextualTutorialAcknowledged;
00066:                 _tutorialPanel.OnContextualAcknowledged += OnContextualTutorialAcknowledged;
00067:             }
00068:
00069:             _onboardingJourney.OnJourneyChanged += journey =>
00070:             {
00071:                 _onboardingDirty = true;
00072:                 _onboardingHintPanel?.Bind(journey);
00073:                 RefreshOnboardingStatusBar();
00074:             };
00075:             _onboardingJourney.OnContextualTutorialRequested += tutorialId =>
00076:             {
00077:                 _onboardingDirty = true;
00078:                 FlushContextualTutorialQueue();
00079:             };
00080:             FlushContextualTutorialQueue();
00081:             // Day tick after a load: read live day and reconcile.
00082:             _onboardingJourney.SetDay(Math.Max(1, _simDay));
00083:             RefreshOnboardingStatusBar();
00084:         }
00085:
00086:         private void EnsureOnboardingPanel()
00087:         {
00088:             if (_onboardingHintPanel != null && _onboardingHintPanel.IsInsideTree())
00089:                 return;
00090:             var panel = new OnboardingHintPanel();
00091:             panel.OnShowMeWhereRequested += route => OpenPlayerPanel(route);
00092:             panel.OnCurrentStepSkipped += SkipCurrentStage;
00093:             panel.OnJourneyReplayed += ReplayJourney;
00094:             panel.OnHintDismissed += () => RefreshOnboardingStatusBar();
00095:             panel.OnAssistanceChanged += SetOnboardingAssistance;
00096:             AddChild(panel);
00097:             _onboardingHintPanel = panel;
00098:         }
00099:
00100:         private void FlushContextualTutorialQueue()
00101:         {
00102:             if (_onboardingJourney == null || _tutorialPanel == null) return;
00103:             if (_onboardingJourney.ContextualTutorialQueue.Count == 0) return;
00104:             if (_tutorialPanel.IsContextualLessonVisible) return;
00105:             string tutorialId = _onboardingJourney.ContextualTutorialQueue[0];
00106:             _tutorialPanel.ShowContextual(tutorialId);
00107:         }
00108:
00109:         private void OnContextualTutorialAcknowledged(string tutorialId)
00110:         {
00111:             if (_onboardingJourney == null) return;
00112:             if (_onboardingJourney.AcknowledgeContextualTutorial(tutorialId))
00113:                 _onboardingDirty = true;
00114:             FlushContextualTutorialQueue();
00115:         }
00116:
00117:         private void RestoreOnboardingFromDisk()
00118:         {
00119:             // Tear down any prior handle and reload from the campaign envelope.
00120:             _onboardingJourney = null;
00121:             _onboardingDirty = false;
00122:             SetupOnboarding();
00123:         }
00124:
00125:         private void SaveOnboarding()
00126:         {
00127:             if (_onboardingJourney == null) return;
00128:             var section = "onboarding";
00129:             var payload = OnboardingSaveStore.TryCapturePersisted(_onboardingJourney.CaptureState());
00130:             if (CaptureSection(section, payload))
00131:             {
00132:                 _onboardingDirty = false;
00133:                 GD.Print("[Ashfall Godot] Onboarding save written.");
00134:             }
00135:         }
00136:
00137:         private void FlushOnboardingIfDirty()
00138:         {
00139:             if (_onboardingDirty) SaveOnboarding();
00140:         }
00141:
00142:         // ── Observation hooks (substep 4) ────────────────────────────────
00143:
00144:         /// <summary>Records a real, reproducible sigil against a genuine
00145:         /// runtime command. The tracker's argument is a stable ID that the
00146:         /// spec promises will never be silently remapped.</summary>
00147:         public void ObserveSigil(string sigil)
00148:         {
00149:             if (_onboardingJourney == null) SetupOnboarding();
00150:             if (_onboardingJourney == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sigil)) return;
00151:             _onboardingJourney.RecordSigil(sigil);
00152:             // Plan 46 — the canonical onboarding/UI sigil seam also feeds the
00153:             // local play-session recorder; the JSONL stream stays an audit read
00154:             // model and never feeds gameplay.
00155:             RecordPlayMetricSigil(sigil);
00156:             _onboardingLastInteractionSeconds = 0;
00157:         }
00158:
00159:         public void ObserveFailedAction(string label)
00160:         {
00161:             _onboardingFailedActions++;
00162:             _onboardingLastInteractionSeconds = 0;
00163:             // Hints are surfacing controlled solely by the hint panel; the
00164:             // host signals the panel that something failed so it can offer
00165:             // a contextual cue on next refresh.
00166:             _onboardingHintPanel?.RefreshView();
00167:         }
00168:
00169:         private void ObserveCurrentDay() => _onboardingJourney?.SetDay(_simDay);
00170:
00171:         // ── Recovery affordances (substep 7) ─────────────────────────────
00172:
00173:         public void SkipCurrentStage()
00174:         {
00175:             if (_onboardingJourney == null) return;
00176:             _onboardingJourney.SkipCurrent();
00177:         }
00178:
00179:         public void SkipAllOnboardingStages()
00180:         {
00181:             _onboardingJourney?.SkipAllRemaining();
00182:         }
00183:
00184:         public void ReplayJourney()
00185:         {
00186:             _onboardingJourney?.Replay();
00187:         }
00188:
00189:         private void ResetOnboardingJourney()
00190:         {
00191:             if (_onboardingJourney == null)
00192:                 SetupOnboarding();
00193:             _onboardingJourney?.Replay();
00194:             _onboardingHintPanel?.RefreshView();
00195:         }
00196:
00197:         private void ApplyOnboardingSettings(Ashfall.Core.Settings.UserSettingsData settings)
00198:         {
00199:             // Plan 184 Path α — refresh owner for preference-sensitive surfaces.
00200:             RefreshAccessibilityPreferenceSurfaces();
00201:
00202:             if (settings.TutorialMode == 2)
00203:             {
00204:                 if (_onboardingHintPanel != null)
00205:                     _onboardingHintPanel.Visible = false;
00206:                 _onboardingJourney = null;
00207:                 _onboardingDirty = false;
00208:                 return;
00209:             }
00210:
00211:             if (_onboardingJourney == null)
00212:                 SetupOnboarding();
00213:             _onboardingHintPanel?.RefreshView();
00214:         }
00215:
00216:         /// <summary>
00217:         /// Re-renders open panels that cache hazard/dose presentation so
00218:         /// <c>hazard_text_labels</c> (and later preference-aware text) stay truthful
00219:         /// after APPLY &amp; SAVE. Engine scale/modulate already applied in
00220:         /// <see cref="Settings.UserSettingsStore.Apply"/>.
00221:         /// </summary>
00222:         private void RefreshAccessibilityPreferenceSurfaces()
00223:         {
00224:             _doseLedgerPanel?.RefreshView();
00225:             _radiationHistoryPanel?.RefreshView();
00226:             // Plan 51 — the presentation motion profile is preference-sensitive.
00227:             RefreshHoldfastPresentation();
00228:         }
00229:
00230:         public void DismissOnboardingHint()
00231:         {
00232:             _onboardingHintPanel?.MarkHintDismissed();
00233:         }
00234:
00235:         public void SetOnboardingAssistance(OnboardingAssistance level)
00236:         {
00237:             if (_onboardingJourney != null)
00238:             {
00239:                 _onboardingJourney.SetAssistance(level);
00240:                 _onboardingDirty = true;
00241:             }
00242:             _onboardingHintPanel?.CycleAssistance(level);
00243:         }
00244:
00245:         public bool HasShowMeWhereOffered(OnboardingStage stage) =>
00246:             _onboardingJourney?.HasShownShowMeWhere(stage) ?? false;
00247:
00248:         public void RecordShowMeWhere(OnboardingStage stage) =>
00249:             _onboardingJourney?.RecordShowMeWhere(stage);
00250:
00251:         /// <summary>Surfaces the current objective unobtrusively in the status
00252:         /// label so a new player sees a reminder even when the hint panel is
00253:         /// not open. Restful, never modal.</summary>
00254:         private void RefreshOnboardingStatusBar()
00255:         {
00256:             if (_onboardingJourney == null || _statusLabel == null) return;
00257:             var j = _onboardingJourney;
00258:             if (j.JourneyComplete) return;
00259:             var def = j.CurrentStageDef;
00260:             // Append rather than overwrite so the existing daily briefing
00261:             // line stays visible.
00262:             string stageId = def.Id switch
00263:             {
00264:                 OnboardingStage.InventoryUse => "inventory_use",
00265:                 OnboardingStage.DayAdvance => "day_advance",
00266:                 _ => def.Id.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()
00267:             };
00268:             string title = AshfallLocalization.Tr($"onboarding.{stageId}.title", def.Title);
00269:             string objective = AshfallLocalization.Tr($"onboarding.{stageId}.objective", def.Objective);
00270:             _statusLabel.Text = AshfallLocalization.TrFormat(
00271:                 "onboarding.status.bar", _simDay, title, objective);
00272:         }
00273:     }
00274: }

00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // ============================================================================
00003: // Plan 184 — Accessibility Options System host wiring.
00004: // Pure domain AccessibilitySettingsSystem governs visual, hearing, motor, and
00005: // cognitive profiles, font scaling, high contrast, and colorblind modes.
00006: // Bridges with UserSettingsStore as the single engine-level user preference authority.
00007: // ============================================================================
00008:
00009: using System;
00010: using Godot;
00011: using Ashfall.Core.Accessibility;
00012:
00013: namespace AtomicWar.GodotApp
00014: {
00015:     public partial class Main
00016:     {
00017:         private AccessibilitySettingsHostSession? _accessibilitySettings;
00018:         private bool _accessibilitySettingsDirty;
00019:
00020:         public AccessibilitySettingsHostSession? AccessibilitySettings => _accessibilitySettings;
00021:
00022:         public void SetupAccessibilitySettings()
00023:         {
00024:             if (_accessibilitySettings != null) return;
00025:
00026:             var saved = AccessibilitySettingsSaveStore.TryLoad();
00027:             _accessibilitySettings = AccessibilitySettingsHostSession.Create(saved);
00028:             _accessibilitySettings.StateChanged += () => _accessibilitySettingsDirty = true;
00029:
00030:             // Load authored accessibility profiles catalog
00031:             string catalogPath = CatalogPath.ResolveCatalog("accessibility_profiles.json");
00032:             var catalogIo = CatalogPath.CreateFileIOForDataDir(CatalogPath.ResolveDataDir());
00033:             if (catalogIo.FileExists(catalogPath))
00034:             {
00035:                 _accessibilitySettings.LoadCatalog(catalogIo.ReadAllText(catalogPath));
00036:             }
00037:         }
00038:
00039:         public bool ApplyAccessibilityProfile(string profileId)
00040:         {
00041:             SetupAccessibilitySettings();
00042:             return _accessibilitySettings!.ApplyProfile(profileId);
00043:         }
00044:
00045:         public void SetAccessibilityColorblindMode(string mode)
00046:         {
00047:             SetupAccessibilitySettings();
00048:             _accessibilitySettings!.SetColorblindMode(mode);
00049:         }
00050:
00051:         public void SetAccessibilityFontScale(float scale)
00052:         {
00053:             SetupAccessibilitySettings();
00054:             _accessibilitySettings!.SetFontScale(scale);
00055:         }
00056:
00057:         public void SetAccessibilityHighContrast(bool enabled)
00058:         {
00059:             SetupAccessibilitySettings();
00060:             _accessibilitySettings!.SetHighContrast(enabled);
00061:         }
00062:
00063:         public void SetAccessibilityReducedMotion(bool enabled)
00064:         {
00065:             SetupAccessibilitySettings();
00066:             _accessibilitySettings!.SetReducedMotion(enabled);
00067:         }
00068:
00069:         public AccessibilityCensus GetAccessibilityCensus() =>
00070:             _accessibilitySettings?.Census ?? default;
00071:
00072:         public void SaveAccessibilitySettings()
00073:         {
00074:             if (_accessibilitySettings == null) return;
00075:             var state = _accessibilitySettings.System.CaptureState();
00076:             AccessibilitySettingsSaveStore.TrySave(state);
00077:             if (CaptureSection(
00078:                     AccessibilitySettingsSaveStore.SectionName,
00079:                     AccessibilitySettingsSaveStore.TryCapturePersisted(state)))
00080:             {
00081:                 _accessibilitySettingsDirty = false;
00082:             }
00083:         }
00084:
00085:         public void FlushAccessibilitySettingsIfDirty()
00086:         {
00087:             if (_accessibilitySettingsDirty)
00088:             {
00089:                 SaveAccessibilitySettings();
00090:             }
00091:         }
00092:
00093:         public void ResetAccessibilitySettings()
00094:         {
00095:             _accessibilitySettings = null;
00096:             _accessibilitySettingsDirty = false;
00097:         }
00098:     }
00099: }
```

01676:

01684:         private void UpdateContinueButton()
01685:         {
01686:             bool hasSave = false;
01687:             if (_saveLoadHost != null)
01688:             {
01689:                 // Continue requires at least one slot that is not terminal. A
01690:                 // run-finalized slot is a sealed memorial/archive, not a
01691:                 // continuable save, so it must not enable Continue.
01692:                 var slots = _saveLoadHost.GetSlots();
01693:                 for (int i = 0; i < slots.Count; i++)
01694:                 {
01695:                     var card = _saveLoadHost.BuildSlotCard(slots[i]);
01696:                     if (card.Exists && !card.IsTerminalIronMan)
01697:                     {
01698:                         hasSave = true;
01699:                         break;
01700:                     }
01701:                 }
01702:             }
01703:             if (!hasSave)
01704:             {
01705:                 // Fall back to legacy global save files.
01706:                 hasSave = System.IO.File.Exists(HoldfastSaveStore.SavePath) ||
01707:                           System.IO.File.Exists(InventorySaveStore.SavePath) ||
01708:                           System.IO.File.Exists(SurvivorsSaveStore.SavePath);
01709:             }
01710:             _mainMenu?.EnableContinue(hasSave);
01711:         }
01712:
01713:         private void OpenStartingCohortSetup()
01714:         {
01715:             if (_state != GameState.Menu || _startingCohortSetupPanel == null) return;
01716:             var catalog = EnsureStartingCohortCatalog();
01717:             _startingCohortSetupPanel.Bind(
01718:                 catalog,
01719:                 EnsureStartingSuppliesCatalog(),
01720:                 EnsureDifficultyCatalog());
01721:             _mainMenu.Visible = false;
01722:             _startingCohortSetupPanel.Open(_startingSuppliesProfileId, _difficultyPresetId);
01723:         }
01724:
01725:         private void CloseStartingCohortSetup()
01726:         {
01727:             _startingCohortSetupPanel?.Close();
01728:             if (_state == GameState.Menu)
01729:                 _mainMenu.Visible = true;
01730:         }
01731:
01732:         private void AddMenuButton(string text, Action callback)
01733:         {
01734:             var btn = new Button
01735:             {
01736:                 Text = text,
01737:                 CustomMinimumSize = new Vector2(0, Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.FontSizeBody + Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.SpacingLg)
01738:             };
01739:             btn.AddThemeFontSizeOverride("font_size", Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.FontSizeBody);
01740:             btn.Pressed += callback;
01741:             _menuContainer.AddChild(btn);
01742:         }
01743:
01744:         private void AddSectionHeader(string title)
01745:         {
01746:             var lbl = AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.AshfallUiHelpers.MakeSectionHeader(title);
01747:             lbl.AddThemeColorOverride("font_color", AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.AshfallUiHelpers.ToColor(Ashfall.Core.UI.Theme.Warm));
01748:             _menuContainer.AddChild(lbl);
01749:         }
01750:
01751:         // -----------------------------------------------------------------
01752:         // Journal wiring
01753:         // -----------------------------------------------------------------
01754:     }
01755: }
```

00158:         private void CloseAllOverlayPanels()
00159:         {
00160:             foreach (Control panel in OverlayPanelCatalog())
00161:             {
00162:                 if (panel == null || !panel.Visible || AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.UiMotion.IsClosing(panel))
00163:                     continue;
00164:
00165:                 AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.AshfallFocusPolicy.RestoreFocusFromRoot(panel);
00166:                 // Central exit transition (UI/UX audit 2026-09-25): every global
00167:                 // dismissal and panel switch fades the old shell out. Falls back
00168:                 // to an immediate hide when motion is unavailable (headless,
00169:                 // captures, ReducedMotion).
00170:                 if (!AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.UiMotion.AnimateClose(panel))
00171:                     panel.Visible = false;
00172:             }
00173:
00174:             if (_journalBook != null && _journalBook.IsOpen)
00175:             {
00176:                 AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.AshfallFocusPolicy.RestoreFocusFromRoot(_journalBook);
00177:                 _journalBook.Close();
00178:             }
00179:         }
00180:

00182:         {
00183:             if (!AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.UiMotion.AnimateClose(_settingsPanel))

00185:         }

01027:         /// <summary>Last overlay root that became visible during an open call.</summary>

01029:
01030:         /// <summary>
01031:         /// Keyboard/controller alpha usability (UI/UX audit follow-up): when a
01032:         /// panel opens, focus its first interactive control once layout settles,
01033:         /// so D-pad/arrows navigate from inside the panel immediately. Deferred
01034:         /// and guarded — a panel (or modal) that already chose a focus owner
01035:         /// keeps it, and the focus ring only appears for keyboard/controller use.
01036:         /// </summary>
01037:         private static void EnsureInitialFocus(Control panel)
01038:         {
01039:             Callable.From(() =>
01040:             {
01041:                 if (!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(panel) || !panel.Visible)
01042:                     return;
01043:                 var owner = panel.GetViewport()?.GuiGetFocusOwner();
01044:                 if (owner != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(owner) && panel.IsAncestorOf(owner))
01045:                     return; // the panel already focused something deliberately
01046:                 var first = AtomicWar.GodotApp.UI.AshfallFocusPolicy.FindFirstFocusable(panel);
01047:                 if (first != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(first))
01048:                     first.GrabFocus();
01049:             }).CallDeferred();
01050:         }
01051:
01052:         /// <summary>
01053:         /// Route → overlay-root map learned lazily from visibility grants. Used
01054:         /// only to cancel an in-flight exit transition when a route is re-opened
01055:         /// before its close fade completes.
01056:         /// </summary>
01057:         private readonly System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, Control> _openControlById = new();
01058:
01059:         /// <summary>
01060:         /// Host close seam (UI/UX audit 2026-09-25 follow-up): plays the shared
01061:         /// exit transition when motion is available, else hides immediately.
01062:         /// </summary>
01063:         private static void ClosePanelAnimated(Control? panel)
01064:         {
01065:             if (panel == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(panel))
01066:                 return;
01067:             if (!UiMotion.AnimateClose(panel))
01068:                 panel.Visible = false;
01069:         }
01070:
01071:         /// <summary>Closes a route's control with the shared exit transition when known.</summary>
01072:         private void CloseRouteAnimated(string targetId)
01073:         {
01074:             if (_openControlById.TryGetValue(targetId, out var control) &&
01075:                 control != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(control) &&
01076:                 UiMotion.AnimateClose(control))
01077:             {
01078:                 return;
01079:             }
01080:             PanelRegistry.TryClose(targetId);
01081:         }
01082:
01083:         /// <summary>
01084:         /// Routes a promoted prototype console id to the live surface that owns
01085:         /// its domain (UI/UX audit 2026-09-25). Opening re-enters the canonical
01086:         /// OpenPlayerPanel path so the target opens exactly as it would from its
01087:         /// own navigation entry (availability, menu gating, and fallback wiring
01088:         /// all apply); the AudioManager cue cooldown absorbs the second confirm.
01089:         /// </summary>
01090:         private void RedirectPrototypeRoute(string sourceId, string targetId)
01091:         {
01092:             PanelRegistry.ConfigureActions(sourceId,
01093:                 openAction: () => OpenPlayerPanel(targetId),
01094:                 closeAction: () => CloseRouteAnimated(targetId));
01095:         }
01096:
01097:         /// <summary>
01098:         /// Live dosimeter selection for the geiger calibration surface: the
01099:         /// ordinal-first device tag registered in the campaign dose ledger, or an
01100:         /// explicit empty selection when no device is registered. Never a demo
01101:         /// tag (INV-16.4); the panel renders its explicit no-device state when
01102:         /// the selection is empty (N16.6).
01103:         /// </summary>
01104:         private string ResolveLiveDosimeterTag()
01105:         {
01106:             var devices = _doseLedger?.Calibration?.Devices;
01107:             if (devices == null || devices.Count == 0) return string.Empty;
01108:             string selected = string.Empty;
01109:             foreach (var tag in devices.Keys)
01110:             {
01111:                 if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(selected) || string.CompareOrdinal(tag, selected) < 0)
01112:                     selected = tag;
01113:             }
01114:             return selected;
01115:         }
01116:
01117:         /// <summary>
01118:         /// Live signal selection for the triangulation surface: the
01119:         /// ordinal-first active distress signal in the campaign radio, or an
01120:         /// explicit empty selection when no signal is active. Never a canned
01121:         /// signal id (INV-16.4); the panel renders its explicit no-signal state
01122:         /// when the selection is empty (N16.6).
01123:         /// </summary>
01124:         private string ResolveLiveTriangulationSignalId()
01125:         {
01126:             var signals = _radio?.DistressSystem?.ActiveSignals;
01127:             if (signals == null) return string.Empty;
01128:             string selected = string.Empty;
01129:             foreach (var signal in signals)
01130:             {
01131:                 if (signal == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(signal.SignalId)) continue;
01132:                 if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(selected) || string.CompareOrdinal(signal.SignalId, selected) < 0)
01133:                     selected = signal.SignalId;
01134:             }
01135:             return selected;
01136:         }
01137:
01138:         private ResearchHostSession? _researchHostSession;
01139:         private StandingRecordHostSession? _standingRecordHostSession;
01140:     }
01141: }

00002: using System;

00010:     /// <summary>
00011:     /// Engine-agnostic unit tests for <see cref="OnboardingJourney"/>.
00012:     /// Validates stage machine, signal semantics, persistence roundtrip,

00015:     /// </summary>
00016:     public class OnboardingJourneyTests
00017:     {

00033:         [Fact]
00034:         public void FreshJourney_StartsAtProtocolStage()
00035:         {
00036:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00037:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingStage.Protocol, j.CurrentStage);
00038:             Assert.False(j.JourneyComplete);

00040:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingAssistance.Standard, j.Assistance);
00041:         }

00107:             // completion is never fabricated.

00109:             {

00111:                 profile = (int)OnboardingProfile.FirstHour,

00126:             Assert.False(restored.IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage.Dose));
00127:             Assert.False(restored.JourneyComplete);

00402:         [Fact]
00403:         public void CaptureState_DefensivelyCopiesCollections()
00404:         {
00405:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00406:             j.RecordSigil(S_ProtocolRation);
00407:             // Capture before we record the second sigil — the captured snapshot
00408:             // is honoured after subsequent live mutations.
00409:             var firstCount = j.Sigils.Count;
00410:             Assert.Equal(1, firstCount);
00411:             var snap = j.CaptureState();
00412:             // The captured snapshot's own sigil collection and the live
00413:             // Dictionary must be independent fields after Capture.
00414:             Assert.Equal(1, snap.sigils.Count);
00415:             Assert.Equal(1, snap.sigils[0].count);
00416:
00417:             // Mutate the live journey after capturing the snapshot. The
00418:             // captured snapshot's sigil list must NOT mirror live state because
00419:             // the persisted list is built ordinally at Capture and the live
00420:             // runtime counter map is not aliased through Capture.
00421:             j.RecordSigil(S_ProtocolMaintenance);
00422:             Assert.Equal(1, snap.sigils.Count);
00423:             Assert.Equal(2, j.Sigils.Count);
00424:             // Restore from the captured snapshot into a new journey — the
00425:             // counter map for the maintenance sigil is absent (it didn't exist
00426:             // at Capture-time).
00427:             var j2 = OnboardingJourney.Restore(snap);
00428:             Assert.Equal(1, j2.Sigils.Count);
00429:             Assert.False(j2.Sigils.ContainsKey(S_ProtocolMaintenance));
00430:         }
00431:
00432:         [Fact]
00433:         public void Restore_ProducesEqualJourneyState_AndIsDeterministic()
00434:         {
00435:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00436:             AdvanceTo(j, OnboardingStage.InventoryUse);
00437:             j.SetAssistance(OnboardingAssistance.Guided);
00438:             j.DismissHint("stage.inventory_use.hint");
00439:
00440:             var a = j.CaptureState();
00441:             var j2 = OnboardingJourney.Restore(a);
00442:
00443:             Assert.Equal(j.CurrentStage, j2.CurrentStage);
00444:             Assert.Equal(j.JourneyComplete, j2.JourneyComplete);
00445:             Assert.Equal(j.Assistance, j2.Assistance);
00446:             Assert.Equal(j.Sigils.Count, j2.Sigils.Count);
00447:             foreach (var key in j.Sigils.Keys)
00448:                 Assert.Equal(j.Sigils[key], j2.Sigils[key]);
00449:             Assert.Equal(j.DismissedHints.Count, j2.DismissedHints.Count);
00450:
00451:             // Determinism: replaying the same capture yields the same state.
00452:             var b = j.CaptureState();
00453:             var j3 = OnboardingJourney.Restore(b);
00454:             for (int i = 0; i < a.completedStages.Count; i++)
00455:                 Assert.Equal(a.completedStages[i], b.completedStages[i]);
00456:         }

00458:         [Fact]
00459:         public void Restore_AfterSave_ResumesAtCorrectStep()
00460:         {
00461:             // Stage 5 (Weather) in flight when saved; restore must surface back
00462:             // to the same outstanding stage — the DoD "resumes at the correct
00463:             // step after save/load" hinge.
00464:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00465:             AdvanceTo(j, OnboardingStage.Weather);
00466:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingStage.Weather, j.CurrentStage);
00467:             Assert.False(j.IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage.Weather));
00468:
00469:             var snap = j.CaptureState();
00470:             var j2 = OnboardingJourney.Restore(snap);
00471:
00472:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingStage.Weather, j2.CurrentStage);
00473:             Assert.False(j2.IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage.Weather));
00474:             Assert.Equal(j.Sigils.Count, j2.Sigils.Count);
00475:         }
00476:
00477:         [Fact]
00478:         public void Restore_AfterEarlierStagesSatisfied_AdvancesCurrentStageCorrectly()
00479:         {
00480:             // Three stages done, persisted with weather.read=0. Restore must
00481:             // NOT mark Weather complete (no signal at save time). Adding the
00482:             // signal after restore must complete inspect / assign / weather in
00483:             // order — the persistent sigils persist, the resume lands at
00484:             // weather.
00485:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00486:             AdvanceTo(j, OnboardingStage.InventoryUse);
00487:             // Don't record weather.read; the current stage is Weather.
00488:             // But AdvanceTo populates InventoryUse sigil so we're already past
00489:             // Weather. Drive backward via a fresh journey instead.
00490:             var fresh = new OnboardingJourney();
00491:             AdvanceTo(fresh, OnboardingStage.Assignment);
00492:             // Assignment requires duty.assigned=1; stage now current. Record
00493:             // inventory.used after restore to progress through the back of
00494:             // the catalog — note that InventoryUse is AFTER Assignment.
00495:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingStage.Assignment, fresh.CurrentStage);
00496:             var snap = fresh.CaptureState();
00497:
00498:             var fresh2 = OnboardingJourney.Restore(snap);
00499:             Assert.Equal(OnboardingStage.Assignment, fresh2.CurrentStage);
00500:             Assert.False(fresh2.IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage.Assignment));
00501:             fresh2.RecordSigil("duty.assigned");
00502:             // Now moves through all stages that just need the missing later
00503:             // signales. Without separate per-stage preclude (we allow
00504:             // later-stage signals to count), duty.assigned as a sigil is now
00505:             // recorded. Assignment's requirement is just duty.assigned=1.
00506:             Assert.True(fresh2.IsStageComplete(OnboardingStage.Assignment));
00507:             // The journey must remain faithful: at the resume point, the
00508:             // already-satisfied earlier stages are still satisfied.
00509:             for (int i = 0; i <= (int)OnboardingStage.Assignment; i++)
00510:                 Assert.True(fresh2.IsStageComplete((OnboardingStage)i));
00511:         }

00555:
00556:         [Fact]
00557:         public void Journey_NeverFabricates_ResourcesOrState()
00558:         {
00559:             // The journey exposes only RecordSigil/SetDay/Restore—none of which
00560:             // can produce inventory/duty/survivor changes. The test asserts the
00561:             // surface contains no mutation methods escaping the journey.
00562:             var journeyMethods = typeof(OnboardingJourney).GetMethods()
00563:                 .Where(m => m.DeclaringType == typeof(OnboardingJourney) &&
00564:                             !m.IsSpecialName)
00565:                 .Select(m => m.Name)
00566:                 .ToHashSet(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00567:
00568:             string[] allowed =
00569:             {
00570:                 "Restore", "CaptureState", "RecordSigil", "SetDay",
00571:                 "SkipCurrent", "SkipAllRemaining", "Replay",
00572:                 "SetAssistance", "DismissHint", "RecordShowMeWhere"
00573:             };
00574:             foreach (var verb in allowed) Assert.Contains(verb, journeyMethods);
00575:
00576:             Assert.DoesNotContain("AddItem", journeyMethods);
00577:             Assert.DoesNotContain("RemoveItem", journeyMethods);
00578:             Assert.DoesNotContain("AssignDuty", journeyMethods);
00579:             Assert.DoesNotContain("AdvanceDay", journeyMethods);
00580:             Assert.DoesNotContain("EquipItem", journeyMethods);
00581:         }
00582:
00583:         [Fact]
00584:         public void CaptureState_AcrossFullJourney_YieldsNonNullCollections()
00585:         {
00586:             var j = new OnboardingJourney();
00587:             // Drive all data stages first so the sigil dictionary is non-empty,
00588:             // THEN tick the day so DayAdvance completes (RecordSigil becomes a
00589:             // no-op once JourneyComplete=true).
00590:             AdvanceTo(j, OnboardingStage.InventoryUse);
00591:             j.SetDay(2);
00592:             var s = j.CaptureState();
00593:             Assert.NotNull(s.sigils);
00594:             Assert.NotNull(s.completedStages);
00595:             Assert.NotNull(s.dismissedHints);
00596:             Assert.NotNull(s.stagesGuided);
00597:             Assert.True(s.sigils.Count > 0);
00598:             Assert.Contains((int)OnboardingStage.DayAdvance, s.completedStages);
00599:         }
00600:
00601:         // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
00602:
00603:         /// <summary>
00604:         /// Pushes the journey to <paramref name="target"/> by emitting the
00605:         /// full set of required sigils for every earlier stage. The helper
00606:         /// stops precisely at <paramref name="target"/>: the stage's own
00607:         /// final precondition is left to the caller's records. The trailing
00608:         /// <c>params string[]</c> keeps the call signature readable but is
00609:         /// intentionally ignored — the core path is canonical and exhaustively
00610:         /// tested by every call site.
00611:         /// </summary>
00612:         private static void AdvanceTo(OnboardingJourney j, OnboardingStage target, params string[] _unused)
00613:         {
00614:             var perStageSigs = new[] {
00615:                 new[] { S_ProtocolRation, S_ProtocolMaintenance, S_ProtocolRadio },
00616:                 new[] { S_InspectRoom, S_InspectRoom, S_InspectRoom },
00617:                 new[] { S_StoreOpened },
00618:                 new[] { S_DutyAssigned },
00619:                 new[] { S_WeatherRead },
00620:                 new[] { S_InventoryUsed },
00621:             };
00622:             int emit = (int)target;
00623:             for (int s = 0; s < emit; s++)
00624:             {
00625:                 if (j.CurrentStage >= target) return;
00626:                 if (j.JourneyComplete) return;
00627:                 foreach (var sig in perStageSigs[s]) j.RecordSigil(sig);
00628:             }
00629:         }
00630:     }
00631: }
```
