```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // ASHFALL CI Gate: First-Hour Onboarding Wiring (UI/UX audit 2026-09-25 follow-up).
00003: //
00004: // The live first-hour journey completes only from real recorded commands
00005: // ("sigils"). This gate is the scripted-playtest backstop: it fails if a live
00006: // stage's required sigil has no producer in production source, if a stage
00007: // ships without hint copy or a show-me-where route, or if an event-driven
00008: // contextual lesson has no authored presentation. Those are exactly the silent
00009: // failures a playtest experiences as "the hint never appears".
00010: using System;
00011: using System.Collections.Generic;
00012: using System.IO;
00013: using System.Linq;
00014: using Xunit;
00015: using Ashfall.Core.Localization;
00016: using Ashfall.Core.Onboarding;
00017:
00018: namespace Ashfall.Core.Tests.UI
00019: {
00020:     public sealed class OnboardingWiringGateTests
00021:     {
00022:         private static string FindRepoRoot()
00023:         {
00024:             var dir = new DirectoryInfo(Path.GetFullPath(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory()));
00025:             while (dir != null)
00026:             {
00027:                 if (Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "src"))
00028:                     && Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "Assets")))
00029:                     return dir.FullName;
00030:                 dir = dir.Parent!;
00031:             }
00032:             throw new DirectoryNotFoundException(
00033:                 "Could not locate repository root from " + Directory.GetCurrentDirectory());
00034:         }
00035:
00036:         private static string AllProductionSource()
00037:         {
00038:             string root = FindRepoRoot();
00039:             var files = Directory.GetFiles(Path.Combine(root, "src"), "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
00040:                 .Where(f => !f.Contains($"{Path.DirectorySeparatorChar}obj{Path.DirectorySeparatorChar}"))
00041:                 .Where(f => !f.Contains($"{Path.DirectorySeparatorChar}bin{Path.DirectorySeparatorChar}"))
00042:                 // HostCli.* files are selftest/dev surfaces, not live producers.
00043:                 .Where(f => !Path.GetFileName(f).StartsWith("HostCli", StringComparison.Ordinal))
00044:                 .Where(f => !Path.GetFileName(f).EndsWith("SelfTest.cs", StringComparison.Ordinal));
00045:             return string.Join("\n", files.Select(File.ReadAllText));
00046:         }
00047:
00048:         [Fact]
00049:         public void EveryFirstHourStageSigil_HasAProductionRecorder()
00050:         {
00051:             string source = AllProductionSource();
00052:             var missing = new List<string>();
00053:
00054:             foreach (var stage in OnboardingCatalog.FirstHourOrder)
00055:             {
00056:                 var def = OnboardingCatalog.DefFor(OnboardingProfile.FirstHour, stage);
00057:                 foreach (var requirement in def.Requirements)
00058:                 {
00059:                     if (!source.Contains($"ObserveSigil(\"{requirement.Sigil}\")", StringComparison.Ordinal))
00060:                         missing.Add($"{stage}:{requirement.Sigil}");
00061:                 }
00062:             }
00063:
00064:             Assert.True(missing.Count == 0,
00065:                 "First-hour stage sigils with no production ObserveSigil(...) call: "
00066:                 + string.Join(", ", missing));
00067:         }
00068:
00069:         [Fact]
00070:         public void EveryFirstHourStage_HasHintCopyAndShowMeWhereRoute()
00071:         {
00072:             foreach (var stage in OnboardingCatalog.FirstHourOrder)
00073:             {
00074:                 var def = OnboardingCatalog.DefFor(OnboardingProfile.FirstHour, stage);
00075:                 Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(def.Title),
00076:                     $"stage {stage} has no hint title");
00077:                 Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(def.Objective),
00078:                     $"stage {stage} has no hint objective");
00079:                 Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(def.ShowMeWhereRoute),
00080:                     $"stage {stage} has no show-me-where route");
00081:             }
00082:         }
00083:
00084:         [Fact]
00085:         public void ContextualLessons_HaveAuthoredPresentation()
00086:         {
00087:             string root = FindRepoRoot();
00088:             string tutorial = File.ReadAllText(
00089:                 Path.Combine(root, "src", "UI", "TutorialPanel.cs"));
00090:
00091:             // The presentation switch must handle each lesson identity (the
00092:             // body fallback is the authored copy; no silent generic default).
00093:             Assert.Contains("OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId", tutorial, StringComparison.Ordinal);
00094:             Assert.Contains("OnboardingLessonLocalization.SevereWeatherPrepId", tutorial, StringComparison.Ordinal);
00095:
00096:             // And each identity starts from a non-empty trigger constant.
00097:             Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId));
00098:             Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(OnboardingLessonLocalization.SevereWeatherPrepId));
00099:         }
00100:
00101:         [Fact]
00102:         public void ContextualLessons_FireAtMostOncePerCampaign()
00103:         {
00104:             var journey = OnboardingJourney.CreateFirstHour();
00105:
00106:             Assert.True(journey.RequestContextualTutorial(
00107:                 OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId));
00108:             Assert.False(journey.RequestContextualTutorial(
00109:                 OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId));
00110:             Assert.True(journey.RequestContextualTutorial(
00111:                 OnboardingLessonLocalization.SevereWeatherPrepId));
00112:
00113:             Assert.Equal(2, journey.ContextualTutorialQueue.Count);
00114:             Assert.True(journey.AcknowledgeContextualTutorial(
00115:                 OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId));
00116:             Assert.Single(journey.ContextualTutorialQueue);
00117:             Assert.False(journey.AcknowledgeContextualTutorial(
00118:                 OnboardingLessonLocalization.ProtectionBeforeDispatchId));
00119:         }
00120:     }
00121: }
```

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // Plan 37 — Input map contract tests (P1 gate mirror).
00003: using System;
00004: using System.Collections.Generic;
00005: using System.IO;
00006: using System.Linq;
00007: using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
00008: using Ashfall.Core.UI;
00009: using Xunit;
00010:
00011: namespace Ashfall.Core.Tests.Tooling
00012: {
00013:     public sealed class InputMapContractTests
00014:     {
00015:         private static string RepoRoot()
00016:         {
00017:             string dir = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory).FullName;
00018:             for (int i = 0; i < 8 && dir != null; i++)
00019:             {
00020:                 if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(dir, "project.godot"))) return dir;
00021:                 dir = Directory.GetParent(dir)?.FullName;
00022:             }
00023:             throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("repo root not found from test context");
00024:         }
00025:
00026:         private static string Read(string relative) =>
00027:             File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(RepoRoot(), relative.Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)));
00028:
00029:         private static HashSet<string> GetProjectGodotActions()
00030:         {
00031:             string content = Read("project.godot");
00032:             var actions = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00033:             var match = Regex.Match(content, @"\[input\](?<inputSection>.*?)(\n\[|\z)", RegexOptions.Singleline);
00034:             if (match.Success)
00035:             {
00036:                 var matches = Regex.Matches(match.Groups["inputSection"].Value, @"(?m)^(ashfall_[a-z0-9_]+)=");
00037:                 foreach (Match m in matches)
00038:                 {
00039:                     actions.Add(m.Groups[1].Value);
00040:                 }
00041:             }
00042:             return actions;
00043:         }
00044:
00045:         private sealed record ContractEntry(string Action, string Scope, string? RouteId, bool Rebindable);
00046:
00047:         private static List<ContractEntry> ParseContractEntries()
00048:         {
00049:             string content = Read("src/Host/AshfallInputActions.cs");
00050:             var entries = new List<ContractEntry>();
00051:
00052:             // Find the Contract array initialization
00053:             var contractBlockMatch = Regex.Match(content, @"public static readonly IReadOnlyList<InputActionContract> Contract\s*=\s*new\[\]\s*\{(?<entries>.*?)\};", RegexOptions.Singleline);
00054:             Assert.True(contractBlockMatch.Success, "Contract array must exist in AshfallInputActions.cs");
00055:
00056:             var rowMatches = Regex.Matches(
00057:                 contractBlockMatch.Groups["entries"].Value,
00058:                 @"new\s+InputActionContract\s*\(\s*(?<action>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*,\s*InputScope\.(?<scope>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*,\s*(?<route>""[^""]*""|null)\s*,\s*(?<rebind>true|false)\s*\)");
00059:
00060:             // Resolve constants
00061:             var constants = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00062:             foreach (Match cm in Regex.Matches(content, @"public const string\s+(?<name>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*""(?<val>[^""]+)"";"))
00063:             {
00064:                 constants[cm.Groups["name"].Value] = cm.Groups["val"].Value;
00065:             }
00066:
00067:             foreach (Match m in rowMatches)
00068:             {
00069:                 string actionIdentifier = m.Groups["action"].Value;
00070:                 string actionName = constants.TryGetValue(actionIdentifier, out var val) ? val : actionIdentifier.Trim('"');
00071:                 string scope = m.Groups["scope"].Value;
00072:                 string rawRoute = m.Groups["route"].Value;
00073:                 string? routeId = rawRoute == "null" ? null : rawRoute.Trim('"');
00074:                 bool rebindable = bool.Parse(m.Groups["rebind"].Value);
00075:
00076:                 entries.Add(new ContractEntry(actionName, scope, routeId, rebindable));
00077:             }
00078:
00079:             return entries;
00080:         }
00081:
00082:         [Fact]
00083:         public void EveryProjectGodotAction_HasContractRow()
00084:         {
00085:             var godotActions = GetProjectGodotActions();
00086:             var contractEntries = ParseContractEntries();
00087:             var contractActionNames = new HashSet<string>(contractEntries.Select(e => e.Action), StringComparer.Ordinal);
00088:
00089:             foreach (var action in godotActions)
00090:             {
00091:                 Assert.True(
00092:                     contractActionNames.Contains(action),
00093:                     $"Action '{action}' is declared in project.godot [input] but is missing a row in AshfallInputActions.Contract.");
00094:             }
00095:         }
00096:
00097:         [Fact]
00098:         public void EveryContractRow_ExistsInProjectGodot()
00099:         {
00100:             var godotActions = GetProjectGodotActions();
00101:             var contractEntries = ParseContractEntries();
00102:
00103:             foreach (var entry in contractEntries)
00104:             {
00105:                 Assert.True(
00106:                     godotActions.Contains(entry.Action),
00107:                     $"Contract row '{entry.Action}' is not declared in project.godot [input].");
00108:             }
00109:         }
00110:
00111:         [Fact]
00112:         public void EveryPredicate_HasCallSiteInSource()
00113:         {
00114:             string actionsCode = Read("src/Host/AshfallInputActions.cs");
00115:             var predicateMatches = Regex.Matches(actionsCode, @"public static bool (Is[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*\(");
00116:             var predicateNames = predicateMatches.Cast<Match>().Select(m => m.Groups[1].Value).Distinct().ToList();
00117:
00118:             Assert.NotEmpty(predicateNames);
00119:
00120:             // Collect all .cs files under src/ except AshfallInputActions.cs
00121:             string repoRoot = RepoRoot();
00122:             string srcDir = Path.Combine(repoRoot, "src");
00123:             var csFiles = Directory.GetFiles(srcDir, "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
00124:                 .Where(f => !Path.GetFileName(f).Equals("AshfallInputActions.cs", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
00125:                 .ToList();
00126:
00127:             var combinedSrc = string.Join("\n", csFiles.Select(File.ReadAllText));
00128:
00129:             foreach (string pred in predicateNames)
00130:             {
00131:                 Assert.True(
00132:                     Regex.IsMatch(combinedSrc, $@"\b{pred}\b"),
00133:                     $"Predicate '{pred}' in AshfallInputActions.cs has 0 call sites across src/ files.");
00134:             }
00135:         }
00136:
00137:         [Fact]
00138:         public void EveryContractRoute_ResolvesInPanelRegistry()
00139:         {
00140:             PanelRegistryBootstrap.RegisterAll();
00141:             var contractEntries = ParseContractEntries();
00142:
00143:             foreach (var entry in contractEntries)
00144:             {
00145:                 if (entry.RouteId != null)
00146:                 {
00147:                     Assert.True(
00148:                         PanelRegistry.IsRegistered(entry.RouteId),
00149:                         $"Action '{entry.Action}' specifies RouteId '{entry.RouteId}', but it is not registered in PanelRegistry.");
00150:                 }
00151:             }
00152:         }
00153:
00154:         [Fact]
00155:         public void CanonicalDefaults_HasNoWithinMapCollision()
00156:         {
00157:             string content = Read("src/Host/AshfallInputActions.cs");
00158:             var defaultsBlockMatch = Regex.Match(content, @"public static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, Key> CanonicalDefaults\s*=\s*new Dictionary<string, Key>\s*\{(?<pairs>.*?)\};", RegexOptions.Singleline);
00159:             Assert.True(defaultsBlockMatch.Success, "CanonicalDefaults must exist in AshfallInputActions.cs");
00160:
00161:             var matches = Regex.Matches(defaultsBlockMatch.Groups["pairs"].Value, @"\{\s*(?<action>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*,\s*Key\.(?<key>[A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*\}");
00162:             var seenKeys = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
00163:             var collisions = new List<string>();
00164:
00165:             foreach (Match m in matches)
00166:             {
00167:                 string action = m.Groups["action"].Value;
00168:                 string key = m.Groups["key"].Value;
00169:
00170:                 if (seenKeys.TryGetValue(key, out var existingAction))
00171:                 {
00172:                     collisions.Add($"Key '{key}' is assigned to both '{existingAction}' and '{action}'");
00173:                 }
00174:                 else
00175:                 {
00176:                     seenKeys[key] = action;
00177:                 }
00178:             }
00179:
00180:             Assert.Empty(collisions);
00181:         }
00182:
00183:         [Fact]
00184:         public void IsConfirmOrAccept_IsDeleted()
00185:         {
00186:             string content = Read("src/Host/AshfallInputActions.cs");
00187:             Assert.DoesNotContain("IsConfirmOrAccept", content);
00188:         }
00189:     }
00190: }
```

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // ASHFALL Core Tests: UI Panel Accessibility Gate Tests.
00003: using System;
00004: using System.IO;
00005: using System.Linq;
00006: using Ashfall.Core;
00007: using Xunit;
00008:
00009: namespace Ashfall.Core.Tests
00010: {
00011:     [IntegrationTest]
00012:     [UnitTest]
00013:     public class UiAccessibilityGateTests
00014:     {
00015:         private static string RepoRoot()
00016:         {
00017:             string[] candidates =
00018:             {
00019:                 Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(),
00020:                 AppContext.BaseDirectory
00021:             };
00022:             foreach (string start in candidates)
00023:             {
00024:                 var dir = new DirectoryInfo(Path.GetFullPath(start));
00025:                 while (dir != null)
00026:                 {
00027:                     string probeProps = Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "Directory.Packages.props");
00028:                     if (File.Exists(probeProps))
00029:                         return dir.FullName;
00030:                     dir = dir.Parent;
00031:                 }
00032:             }
00033:             throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Could not locate repository root from test execution context.");
00034:         }
00035:
00036:         [Fact]
00037:         public void HostCliRegistry_RegistersUiAccessibilitySelfTest()
00038:         {
00039:             var desc = HostCliRegistry.AllDescriptors.FirstOrDefault(d => d.Action == HostCliAction.UiAccessibilitySelfTest);
00040:             Assert.NotNull(desc);
00041:             Assert.Equal("--ui-accessibility-selftest", desc.PrimaryFlag);
00042:             Assert.True(desc.IsSelfTest);
00043:             Assert.True(desc.IsTest);
00044:             Assert.True(desc.HeadlessCompatible);
00045:             Assert.Equal("ui_accessibility_selftest", desc.TestId);
00046:             Assert.Contains("--accessibility-selftest", desc.Aliases);
00047:         }
00048:
00049:         [Fact]
00050:         public void HostCliRegistry_ResolvesAccessibilityFlags()
00051:         {
00052:             Assert.Equal(HostCliAction.UiAccessibilitySelfTest, HostCliRegistry.Resolve(new[] { "--ui-accessibility-selftest" }));
00053:             Assert.Equal(HostCliAction.UiAccessibilitySelfTest, HostCliRegistry.Resolve(new[] { "--accessibility-selftest" }));
00054:             Assert.Equal(HostCliAction.UiAccessibilitySelfTest, HostCliRegistry.Resolve(new[] { "--ui-a11y-selftest" }));
00055:         }
00056:
00057:         [Fact]
00058:         public void UiPanelSourceFiles_DoNotContainCorruptPlaceholders()
00059:         {
00060:             string root = RepoRoot();
00061:             string uiDir = Path.Combine(root, "src", "UI");
00062:
00063:             if (!Directory.Exists(uiDir)) return;
00064:
00065:             var panelFiles = Directory.EnumerateFiles(uiDir, "*Panel*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
00066:                 .Where(f => !f.EndsWith("Test.cs") && !f.EndsWith("Tests.cs"))
00067:                 .ToList();
00068:
00069:             foreach (var file in panelFiles)
00070:             {
00071:                 string text = File.ReadAllText(file);
00072:                 Assert.DoesNotContain("\"[PLACEHOLDER]\"", text);
00073:                 Assert.DoesNotContain("\"[MISSING]\"", text);
00074:             }
00075:         }
00076:     }
00077: }
```

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: using System;

00004: using Ashfall.Core.Settings;
00005: using Xunit;
00006:

00223:         [Fact]
00224:         public void EndToEnd_SaveCorruptRecover_ResilientRoundTrip()
00225:         {
00226:             string path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"ashfall_settings_e2e_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
00227:             try
00228:             {
00229:                 // Phase 1: Save custom settings to a real file.
00230:                 var custom = new UserSettingsData
00231:                 {
00232:                     MasterVolume = 0.42f,
00233:                     MaxFps = 144,
00234:                     HighContrast = true,
00235:                     ResolutionWidth = 2560,
00236:                     ResolutionHeight = 1440
00237:                 };
00238:                 File.WriteAllText(path, UserSettingsCodec.Serialize(custom));
00239:                 Assert.True(File.Exists(path));
00240:
00241:                 // Phase 2: Read back — should match exactly.
00242:                 string raw = File.ReadAllText(path);
00243:                 var (loaded, diag1) = UserSettingsCodec.DeserializeWithRecovery(raw);
00244:                 Assert.Null(diag1);
00245:                 Assert.Equal(0.42f, loaded.MasterVolume);
00246:                 Assert.Equal(144, loaded.MaxFps);
00247:                 Assert.True(loaded.HighContrast);
00248:
00249:                 // Phase 3: Corrupt the file on disk.
00250:                 File.WriteAllText(path, "{ TRUNCATED_CORRUPT");
00251:
00252:                 // Phase 4: Recover — should get safe defaults with diagnostic.
00253:                 string corruptRaw = File.ReadAllText(path);
00254:                 var (recovered, diag2) = UserSettingsCodec.DeserializeWithRecovery(corruptRaw);
00255:                 Assert.NotNull(diag2);
00256:                 Assert.Contains("Invalid settings JSON", diag2);
00257:                 Assert.Equal(1.0f, recovered.MasterVolume);
00258:                 Assert.Equal(60, recovered.MaxFps);
00259:                 Assert.Equal(1920, recovered.ResolutionWidth);
00260:
00261:                 // Phase 5: Re-save recovered defaults, then re-read — clean round-trip.
00262:                 File.WriteAllText(path, UserSettingsCodec.Serialize(recovered));
00263:                 string reread = File.ReadAllText(path);
00264:                 var (final, diag3) = UserSettingsCodec.DeserializeWithRecovery(reread);
00265:                 Assert.Null(diag3);
00266:                 Assert.Equal(1.0f, final.MasterVolume);
00267:                 Assert.Equal(60, final.MaxFps);
00268:             }
00269:             finally
00270:             {
00271:                 if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path);
00272:             }
00273:         }
```

```csharp
00001: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
00002: // ASHFALL accessibility guard test (Plan 80 / Task B21).
00003: // Enforces font floors, focus policy presence, and bans raw Key.Escape
00004: // in migrated top dashboard and overlay panels.
00005:
00006: using System;
00007: using System.IO;
00008: using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
00009: using Xunit;
00010: using Ashfall.Core.UI;
00011:
00012: namespace Ashfall.Core.Tests.UI
00013: {
00014:     public class AccessibilitySourceAuditTests
00015:     {
00016:         private static readonly Regex LineComment = new Regex("//.*", RegexOptions.Compiled);
00017:         private static readonly Regex BlockComment =
00018:             new Regex("/\\*.*?\\*/", RegexOptions.Compiled | RegexOptions.Singleline);
00019:
00020:         private static readonly Regex HardcodedFontSizeBelowFloor =
00021:             new Regex(@"(?:font_size""\s*,\s*|FontSize\s*=\s*)([0-9]+)\b", RegexOptions.Compiled);
00022:
00023:         private static string FindRepoRoot()
00024:         {
00025:             string[] candidates =
00026:             {
00027:                 Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(),
00028:                 AppContext.BaseDirectory
00029:             };
00030:             foreach (string start in candidates)
00031:             {
00032:                 var dir = new DirectoryInfo(Path.GetFullPath(start));
00033:                 while (dir != null)
00034:                 {
00035:                     string probeSrc = Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "src");
00036:                     string probeAssets = Path.Combine(dir.FullName, "Assets");
00037:                     if (Directory.Exists(probeSrc) && Directory.Exists(probeAssets))
00038:                         return dir.FullName;
00039:                     dir = dir.Parent;
00040:                 }
00041:             }
00042:             throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Could not locate repo root from the test run");
00043:         }
00044:
00045:         private static string StripComments(string text)
00046:         {
00047:             text = BlockComment.Replace(text, string.Empty);
00048:             return LineComment.Replace(text, string.Empty);
00049:         }
00050:
00051:         [Fact]
00052:         public void ThemeFontSizes_MeetAccessibilityFloors()
00053:         {
00054:             // Plan 80:
00055:             // - body text never below floor (15px)
00056:             // - label floor (11px)
00057:             // - small floor (12px)
00058:             // - mono floor (13px)
00059:             Assert.True(Theme.FontSizeBody >= 14, "Theme.FontSizeBody must be at least 14px for readable body text.");
00060:             Assert.True(Theme.FontSizeLabel >= 11, "Theme.FontSizeLabel must be at least 11px.");
00061:             Assert.True(Theme.FontSizeSmall >= 12, "Theme.FontSizeSmall must be at least 12px.");
00062:             Assert.True(Theme.FontSizeMono >= 12, "Theme.FontSizeMono must be at least 12px.");
00063:         }
00064:
00065:         [Fact]
00066:         public void UiSourceFiles_HaveNoFontSizesBelowAbsoluteFloor()
00067:         {
00068:             string root = FindRepoRoot();
00069:             string uiDir = Path.Combine(root, "src", "UI");
00070:             Assert.True(Directory.Exists(uiDir), $"UI directory {uiDir} must exist");
00071:
00072:             var csFiles = Directory.GetFiles(uiDir, "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories);
00073:             Assert.NotEmpty(csFiles);
00074:
00075:             foreach (var file in csFiles)
00076:             {
00077:                 string text = File.ReadAllText(file);
00078:                 string clean = StripComments(text);
00079:                 var matches = HardcodedFontSizeBelowFloor.Matches(clean);
00080:                 foreach (Match match in matches)
00081:                 {
00082:                     if (int.TryParse(match.Groups[1].Value, out int size))
00083:                     {
00084:                         Assert.False(size < 11,
00085:                             $"File {Path.GetFileName(file)} contains hardcoded font size {size}px which is below the absolute floor of 11px.");
00086:                     }
00087:                 }
00088:             }
00089:         }
00090:
00091:         [Fact]
00092:         public void MigratedPanels_DoNotUseRawEscapeKey()
00093:         {
00094:             string root = FindRepoRoot();
00095:             string uiDir = Path.Combine(root, "src", "UI");
00096:
00097:             string[] migratedPanels =
00098:             {
00099:                 "InventoryPanel.cs",
00100:                 "ResearchAtlasPanel.cs",
00101:                 "ResearchPanel.cs",
00102:                 "CraftingPanel.cs",
00103:                 "MedicalPanel.cs",
00104:                 "StatusPanel.cs"
00105:             };
00106:
00107:             foreach (var panelFile in migratedPanels)
00108:             {
00109:                 string path = Path.Combine(uiDir, panelFile);
00110:                 Assert.True(File.Exists(path), $"Migrated panel file {path} must exist");
00111:
00112:                 string content = StripComments(File.ReadAllText(path));
00113:                 Assert.DoesNotContain("Key.Escape", content);
00114:             }
00115:         }
00116:
00117:         [Fact]
00118:         public void AshfallFocusPolicy_IsImplementedAndExposesRequiredApi()
00119:         {
00120:             string root = FindRepoRoot();
00121:             string policyPath = Path.Combine(root, "src", "UI", "AshfallFocusPolicy.cs");
00122:             Assert.True(File.Exists(policyPath), "AshfallFocusPolicy.cs must exist in src/UI/");
00123:
00124:             string content = StripComments(File.ReadAllText(policyPath));
00125:             Assert.Contains("MakeFocusVisibleStyleBox", content);
00126:             Assert.Contains("ApplyFocusVisibleStyle", content);
00127:             Assert.Contains("OpenWithFocus", content);
00128:             Assert.Contains("TrapFocus", content);
00129:             Assert.Contains("RestoreFocus", content);
00130:         }
00131:     }
00132: }
```

# Appendix M — External verification handoff

The following checks are to be run by the owning integrator after writing: character count, SHA-256 revalidation, path-token resolution, duplicate-heading/unsupported-claim scan, and `git diff --check`. The final ledger entry must report actual results, not this template.
